Code and dataset: https://huggingface.co/datasets/shaghayegh/ContextBias , https://github.com/Sina-Emami/ContextBias

</details>


### [141] [MariSat: A Maritime Dataset for Instance Segmentation of Objects in Satellite and Aerial Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29852)
*Amir Abbes,Ines Harrabi,Lucas Justin Yirepoa Kinda,Rim Trabelsi,Adnane Cabani,Fatma Abdelkefi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文介绍了MariSat，一个包含1260张航拍和卫星图像的港口及海岸场景实例分割基准数据集，涵盖8类海上目标，通过半自动标注管线（SAM 3结合几何与比色后处理过滤器，以及CVAT人工校正）构建，并已用于SAM 3和YOLO11模型的微调与基准测试。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动化海上监视需要大规模且精确标注的数据集，但现有的实例分割任务数据集（尤其是港口复杂环境中小型船舶）仍然稀缺，难以支撑相关模型的训练与评估。

Method: 采用半自动标注管线：结合文本提示分割模型SAM 3与级联的几何和比色后处理过滤器，再通过CVAT标注平台进行人工校正和质量控制，最终构建MariSat数据集，并划分训练、验证和测试集。

Result: 构建了包含1260张图像、8个海上目标类别（帆船、游艇、摩托艇、渔船、邮轮、军用舰艇、拖船和货船）的像素级标注数据集，报告了各类别的统计结果，并完成了SAM 3和YOLO11的分割与检测基准测试，同时提供定量及逐类结果。

Conclusion: MariSat为海事实例分割与检测研究提供了公开可用的高质量基准数据集，验证了半自动标注管线的有效性，并为实时海上监控应用奠定了基础。

Abstract: Automated maritime surveillance from satellite and aerial imagery requires large, precisely annotated datasets, which remain scarce for the instance-segmentation task, particularly for small vessels in cluttered port environments. We present MariSat, a new benchmark dataset of 1260 aerial and satellite images covering diverse port and coastal scenes, annotated at the pixel level for eight maritime object classes (sailboat, yacht, jet-ski, fishing boat, cruise ship, military vessel, tugboat and cargo ship). The dataset was produced through a semi-automatic annotation pipeline combining the textpromptable segmentation model SAM 3 with a cascade of geometric and colorimetric post-processing filters, followed by a manual correction and quality-control pass performed with the CVAT annotation platform. We describe the image-collection methodology, the annotation and correction process, and the resulting data organization. We also report class-wise statistics for the training, validation, and test splits. MariSat has already been used to fine-tune and benchmark segmentation and detection models (SAM 3 and YOLO11) for real-time maritime monitoring. We report detailed quantitative and per-class results for both tasks. The MariSat dataset is publicly available on GitHub : https://github.com/amirabbes/P2M-Maritime-Segmentation

</details>


### [142] [OptiGeo: Efficient Monocular Geometry for Embodied Perception in Optically Challenging Scenes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29881)
*Muxin Liu,Tianbo Liu,Jing Xia,Xiaoyang Lyu,Xiaoshan Wu,Bo Wang,Peng Dai,Zhongrui Wang,Shaoshuai Shi,Xiaojuan Qi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: OptiGeo是一个偏置感知的训练框架，通过干净几何教师模型和残差修剪对齐来修复传感数据中的偏置监督，仅用3千万参数就在透明场景深度估计上超越了3亿参数的单目模型和十亿级多视角基线，同时保持通用零样本深度估计的竞争力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单目深度估计在开放域泛化上表现良好，但在透明、反射和镜面环境中，深度传感器常产生缺失或偏置数据。现有方法依赖场景特定预处理、辅助模块或事后微调，虽在受限场景有效，但增加了架构冗余，且会使通用几何模型过度专精于狭窄光学场景。作者识别出传感器诱导的监督偏置是核心瓶颈。

Method: OptiGeo框架包含两个关键设计：(1) 使用干净几何教师模型修复有偏置的真实深度监督；(2) 引入残差修剪对齐机制。此外，作者将面向透明目标的渲染重新定义为紧凑的干净光学几何来源，而非大规模特定领域微调数据集，只需小型定向渲染集即可学习透明物体和区域的几何结构。

Result: OptiGeo仅30M参数，在透明场景基准上超越300M参数的更大单目模型和十亿级多视角基线，并在通用零样本深度和边界锐度上保持竞争力。真实世界导航实验验证了其在光学挑战场景中作为高效感知模块的实用性。

Conclusion: 通过将光学失效视为基础模型训练中的局部失败模式，并利用清洁几何监督修复传感器偏置，可以有效解决透明场景下的深度估计问题，无需增加架构复杂度或依赖大规模领域特定的微调数据集。

Abstract: Monocular depth estimation has achieved strong open-domain generalization, yet reliable robotic deployment remains difficult in transparent, reflective, and specular environments, where depth sensors often produce missing or biased depth. Existing methods often handle such optical failures with scene-specific preprocessing, auxiliary modules, or post-hoc fine-tuning. While effective in constrained settings, these designs increase architectural redundancy and can over-specialize general geometry models to narrow optical scenarios. We revisit this problem as a localized failure mode within base-model training and identify sensor-induced supervision bias as a key bottleneck: models inherit sensor failure patterns from biased real-depth supervision in optically challenging regions. We then introduce OptiGeo, a bias-aware training framework that rehabilitates biased real supervision using a clean-geometry teacher and residual-trimmed alignment. We redefine transparency-targeted rendering as a compact source of clean optical geometry, rather than a large domain-specific fine-tuning set. With only a small targeted rendering set, OptiGeo learns the geometric structure of transparent objects and regions, correcting local geometry distortions that real sensors cannot reliably supervise. Despite only 30M parameters, OptiGeo outperforms substantially larger 300M-scale monocular models and billion-scale multi-view baselines on transparent-scene benchmarks, while remaining competitive on general zero-shot depth and boundary sharpness. Real-world navigation cases further validate its practicality as an efficient perception module in optically challenging scenes.

</details>


### [143] [MASQ: Mask-Aware Spatiotemporal Quantization for Unsupervised Skeleton Action Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29891)
*Xinyao Qin,Linxiang Peng,Youbao Ye,Di Yang,Jiangtao Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为MASQ的无监督骨架时序动作分割框架，通过解耦空间特征推理与时间平滑两大冲突任务，引入联合级结构化丢弃（JLSD）和掩码感知速度损失，解决了掩码引入的表征模糊和离散量化放大潜在空间波动导致的动作边界时序抖动问题，在三个基准数据集上取得了领先性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有无监督骨架动作分割方法依赖离散量化发现动作边界，但空间掩码引入表征模糊，离散量化放大潜在空间小波动，两者相互作用导致边界附近码本切换不稳定和严重时序抖动。

Method: 提出MASQ框架，在空间维度引入联合级结构化丢弃（JLSD）机制，掩码所选关节的完整时间轨迹，促进学习判别性关节间协调模式；在时间维度设计掩码感知速度损失，仅对可见关节强制执行运动一致性，防止掩码信号引起的梯度冲突并稳定时序预测。

Result: 在HuGaDB、LARa和BABEL三个公开骨架数据集上的大量实验表明，MASQ显著优于现有无监督SOTA方法，尤其在Mean over Frames准确率指标上建立了全面且显著的领先优势。

Conclusion: 通过解耦空间特征推断与时间平滑任务，并分别采取针对性的掩码策略与损失设计，有效缓解了无监督骨架动作分割中的边界抖动问题，验证了该框架的有效性与通用性。

Abstract: Unsupervised skeleton-based temporal action segmentation is a crucial task for understanding human behavior in long untrimmed sequences. Recent approaches often rely on discrete quantization to discover action boundaries from motion representations. However, when spatial masking is introduced for representation learning, it can introduce representation ambiguity, while discrete quantization further amplifies small fluctuations in the latent space. The interaction between these two factors often leads to unstable code switching and severe temporal jitter near action boundaries.To address these limitations, we propose a novel Mask-aware Action Spatiotemporal Quantization (MASQ) framework. Our framework decouples the conflicting tasks of spatial feature inference and temporal smoothing.In the spatial dimension, we introduce a Joint-Level Structured Dropout (JLSD) mechanism that masks the entire temporal trajectory of selected joints, to encourage the model to learn discriminative inter-joint coordination patterns. In the temporal dimension, we design a mask-aware velocity loss that enforces motion consistency only on visible joints, that prevents gradient conflicts caused by masked signals and stabilizing temporal predictions. Extensive experiments on three widely used skeleton datasets, including HuGaDB, LARa, and BABEL, demonstrate that the proposed MASQ framework significantly outperforms existing state-of-the-art unsupervised methods. In particular, our model establishes a comprehensive and substantial leading advantage in the Mean over Frames accuracy.

</details>


### [144] [Off-Manifold Refinement: Guiding Video Generators with a Frozen World Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29904)
*Hai Nguyen-Truong,Tuan-Anh Vu,Dang Huynh*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种名为Off-Manifold Refinement (OMR)的推理时方法，通过将世界模型反馈直接注入单次采样轨迹的中间步骤，在不增加训练或多次采样成本的情况下，提升视频生成器的物理一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频生成器在物理动力学方面经常失败（物体漂浮、轨迹违反重力、接触消失），因为标准的去噪和流匹配目标只拟合视觉数据分布，不显式惩罚物理违规。现有改进方法要么增加推理/训练成本（如候选选择、基于梯度的世界模型引导、生成器内部精炼、后训练），缺乏高效的解决方案。

Method: OMR在采样的中间ODE步骤中，将适配器空间V-JEPA 2.1"惊讶能量"的梯度注入生成器速度中。该外部修正可使潜伏状态偏离未校正的采样轨迹，朝向被冻结预测器判定为物理上更合理的区域移动，之后生成器从修正状态继续渲染。使用小型潜伏到嵌入适配器保持推理时梯度可计算，视频生成器和世界模型均保持冻结。

Result: 在固定400提示的VideoPhy-2详细子集上，OMR将联合语义保持性和物理常识指标从47.0%提升至52.0%（绝对提升5.0个百分点，相对提升10.6%），超过基准Wan2.2-T2V-A14B采样器。在50提示的效率子集上，只需1.71倍基础运行时间，而非奖励/搜索替代方案的乘法成本。

Conclusion: OMR提供了一种轻量、高效的推理时物理一致性改进方案，通过将冻结世界模型的物理常识直接嵌入单次采样过程，在显著提升物理合理性指标的同时，避免了多次采样或额外训练带来的计算开销。

Abstract: Modern video generators routinely fail at physical dynamics: objects float, trajectories violate gravity, contacts vanish. Standard denoising and flow-matching objectives fit visual data distributions but do not explicitly penalize such physical violations. Existing remedies can improve physical consistency, but typically add substantial inference or training cost. Candidate-selection methods generate and score multiple videos, while gradient-based world-model guidance repeatedly decodes and re-encodes intermediate estimates. Generator-internal refinement adds perturbation and re-denoising loops, whereas post-training requires curated data and additional optimization. We propose Off-Manifold Refinement (OMR), an inference-time method that instead injects world-model feedback directly into a single sampling trajectory. During scheduled middle ODE steps, we augment the generator velocity with the gradient of an adapter-space V-JEPA 2.1 surprise energy. This external correction can move the latent away from the uncorrected sampling trajectory and toward regions ranked as more physically plausible by the frozen predictor, after which the generator continues rendering from the corrected state. A small trained latent-to-embedding adapter keeps the gradient tractable at inference, and both the video generator and the world model remain frozen. On our fixed 400-prompt VideoPhy-2 detailed subset, OMR lifts the joint Semantic-Adherence-and-Physical-Commonsense metric from 47.0% to 52.0% (+5.0pp absolute, +10.6% relative) over the base Wan2.2-T2V-A14B sampler. On a separate fixed 50-prompt efficiency subset, it requires $1.71 \times$ the base runtime rather than the multiplicative cost of reward/search alternatives. Project page: https://itruonghai.github.io/omr.

</details>


### [145] [OrnaStyler: Ornament-Aware Latent Editing for Content-Preserving 3D Stylization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29905)
*Tomohiro Aizawa,Shigeru Kuriyama,Chunzhi Gu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出OrnaStyler，一种零样本的文本引导3D资产风格化框架，基于整流流生成建模，通过分阶段的逆推引导编辑策略恢复内容和外观层面的潜在表征，显式建模风格元素的空间配置，实现精细结构装饰的风格化编辑，同时保持源几何和对象身份，在内容保持、风格保真度和视觉真实性方面达到最先进水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 3D资产风格化在数字内容创作中至关重要，但现有方法在需要对现有资产进行精细结构装饰的风格化时仍面临挑战——需要在保持源几何形状和对象身份的同时，连贯地整合新的风格特定细节。

Method: 采用整流流生成模型；提出逆推引导编辑策略，分阶段恢复几何和外观层面的内容感知潜在表征；在几何层面通过流逆推操作体素表征以合成装饰增强结构，同时保持空间身份；在外观层面引入邻域感知特征修补机制，协调新生成装饰与原始内容的融合。

Result: 在生成和真实世界3D资产的广泛实验表明，OrnaStyler在内容保持、风格保真度和整体视觉真实性方面优于现有方法，达到最先进的编辑性能，且仅需推理阶段操作，支持选择性编辑几何增强或外观风格化。

Conclusion: OrnaStyler通过显式建模风格元素的空间配置，有效缓解了内容保持与风格表达之间的根本张力，实现了高质量的文本引导装饰感知3D风格化编辑。

Abstract: Text-guided style editing of 3D assets is essential for adapting existing objects to diverse visual aesthetics in digital content creation. Despite rapid progress in 3D shape modeling, faithfully stylizing an existing asset remains challenging when the desired stylization involves fine-grained structural ornamentation, which requires the model to preserve the source geometry and object identity, while coherently integrating new style-specific details. We propose \textbf{OrnaStyler}, a zero-shot framework for text-guided ornament-aware 3D stylization. Built upon rectified flow-based generative modeling, OrnaStyler introduces an inversion-guided editing strategy that recovers content-aware latent representations at both geometry and appearance levels in a staged manner to facilitate faithful editing. Our core idea is to explicitly model the spatial configuration of stylistic elements, thereby mitigating the fundamental tension between content preservation and style expression in the voxel space. Specifically, at the geometry level, we manipulate voxel representations through flow inversion to synthesize ornament-enhanced structures while preserving the spatial identity of the source asset. Then, at the appearance level, we introduce an adjacency-aware feature inpainting mechanism to harmonize newly generated ornaments with the original content, yielding coherent geometry-appearance integration. Our approach operates solely in the inference phase and enables selective editing over geometric augmentation or appearance stylization. Extensive experiments on both generated and real-world 3D assets against prior methods demonstrate that OrnaStyler achieves state-of-the-art editing performance in terms of content preservation, style fidelity, and overall visual realism. Code is available at: https://github.com/tomohiro0427/OrnaStyler

</details>


### [146] [Matrix-Game 3.5: Enhancing Real-Time Streaming Interactive World Models with Patch Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29910)
*Runjia Qian,Zile Wang,Jihai Zhang,Kai Zou,Wei Yu,Jiaxing Li,Zexiang Liu,Yaokun Li,Fei Kang,Kaichen Huang,Mengyin An,Haobo Zhang,Biao Jiang,Jiahua Wang,Haofeng Sun,Yang Liu,Yangguang Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Matrix-Game 3.5是一个面向实时交互式世界生成的基础模型，通过几何感知记忆框架、静动态解耦表示和两阶段渐进式实时蒸馏三大改进，实现了分钟级的长时序一致、几何精确且相机可控的实时交互式世界仿真。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 交互式世界模型需要从离线视频片段生成扩展到持久性的虚拟世界实时仿真，但现有方法难以同时保持场景几何、动态一致性和相机控制，且无法支持实时自回归生成。

Method: 提出三个关键改进：(1) 统一的几何感知记忆框架，包含patch-memory和tiled-PRoPE组件，结合显式3D patch检索与投影相机条件化，无需额外可学习参数即可实现几何一致的相机控制和长时序场景回忆；(2) 静动态解耦的世界表示，分别建模静态场景几何与动态主体，保持几何一致性和主体身份；(3) 两阶段渐进式实时蒸馏框架，通过感知流匹配和基于课程的自回放DMD将双向扩散模型转换为少步因果生成器。

Result: 在包含Unreal仿真环境、开放世界游戏和互联网视频的统一训练语料上，实现了长时序场景回忆、精确相机控制、主体一致性、提示驱动世界生成和稳定实时开放世界交互方面的强性能。

Conclusion: Matrix-Game 3.5证明了通过几何感知记忆、静动态解耦表示和高效的蒸馏框架，可以迈向几何感知且长时序一致的实时交互式世界生成。

Abstract: Interactive world models extend video generation from offline clip synthesis toward persistent simulation of interactive virtual worlds, enabling applications in games, robotics, embodied agents, and XR. Achieving stable long-horizon interactive generation, however, remains challenging, as the model must simultaneously preserve scene geometry, dynamic consistency, and camera control while supporting real-time autoregressive generation. Building upon Matrix-Game 3.0, we present Matrix-Game 3.5, as shown in Figure 1, which advances real-time interactive world generation toward geometry-aware and long-horizon consistent simulation through three key improvements. First, we propose a unified geometry-aware memory framework, whose patch-memory and tiled-PRoPE components introduce no additional learnable parameters, combining explicit 3D patch retrieval with projective camera conditioning to enable geometry-consistent camera control and faithful long-horizon scene recall. Second, we introduce a static-dynamic disentangled world representation that separately models static scene geometry and dynamic subjects, preserving both geometric consistency and subject identity throughout long-horizon generation. Third, we develop a two-stage progressive real-time distillation framework that converts a bidirectional diffusion model into a few-step causal generator through Perceptual Flow Matching and curriculum based Self-Rollout DMD, enabling minute-long real-time interactive generation. Extensive experiments demonstrate that, with a unified training corpus spanning Unreal simulation environments, open-world games, and internet videos, MatrixGame 3.5 achieves strong performance in long-horizon scene recall, precise camera control, subject consistency, prompt-driven world generation, and stable real-time open-world interaction.

</details>


### [147] [FoundYou: A Unified Model for Personalized Segmentation and Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29917)
*Gabriele Trivigno,Marcos Alfaro,Claudia Cuttano,Gabriele Berton,Luis Payá,Carlo Masone*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出FoundYou，一个基于SAM 2的统一框架，将个性化分割和个性化检索统一为同一个实例对齐过程，在PerMIS上提升+18.4 mIoU，在ILIAS上提升+17.8 mAP，且模型仅增加5.9M可训练参数，比现有统一方案快75倍、小20倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 个性化分割和个性化检索虽然都旨在跨图像识别同一物理对象，但这两个任务长期以来被独立发展、用不同方案解决，缺乏统一的框架来同时处理这两个共享实例级目标的任务。

Method: 利用SAM 2在视频帧间保持对象身份的特性，保留其冻结的small模型，通过实例对齐过程同时实现分割与检索，只需增加5.9M可训练参数。该方法还支持少样本个性化检索和灵活提示的个性化分割。

Result: 在PerMIS上提升+18.4 mIoU，在ILIAS上提升+17.8 mAP；性能随参考图像数量增加而提升，对较弱提示保持鲁棒；在类别级检索基准上达到SOTA；模型比现有统一方案快75倍以上、小20倍。

Conclusion: FoundYou证明了基于SAM 2的统一框架能够自然地将个性化分割与检索统一为同一实例对齐过程，并带来显著的性能提升和效率优势，开辟了少样本个性化检索和灵活提示分割等新能力。

Abstract: Personalized segmentation and personalized retrieval both aim to identify the same physical object across different images. While the former localizes the object within a target image, the latter retrieves images where it appears. Despite this shared instance-level objective, the two tasks have largely evolved separately and are addressed with distinct solutions. In this work, we introduce FoundYou, a unified framework built on the observation that Segment Anything 2 (SAM 2), trained to preserve object identity across video frames, inherently captures instance-level cues. We leverage this property to match objects across independent images, enabling segmentation and retrieval to emerge as two outcomes of the same instance alignment process. This unified view unlocks new capabilities beyond traditional benchmarks, including few-shot personalized retrieval and promptable personalized segmentation with flexible prompts. Extensive experiments show consistent gains over unified and task-specific methods, including +18.4 mIoU on PerMIS and +17.8 mAP on ILIAS. Performance scales with additional references and remains robust to weaker prompts. Beyond personalization, FoundYou achieves state-of-the-art results on category-level retrieval benchmarks. Notably, our approach keeps the SAM 2-small model entirely frozen and adds only 5.9 M trainable parameters, yielding a 52 M-parameter model that is over 75x faster and 20x smaller than the only prior unified solution. Code is available at https://github.com/ga1i13o/FoundYou .

</details>


### [148] [Continual Test-Time Adaptation via Entropy Sensitivity-Guidance in Strict Online Setting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29920)
*Chandler Timm C. Doloriel,Yunbei Zhang,Muhammad Salman Siddiqui,Tor Kristian Stevik,Fadi Al Machot,Kristian Hovde Liland,Habib Ullah*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出SEGA方法，通过在测试时对样本进行结构化擦除来探测预测熵的变化轨迹，实现严格的在线持续测试时适应（batch size为1、无源数据），有效缓解漂移和崩溃问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 严格的在线TTA（batch size为1、无源数据）容易发生模型漂移或崩溃，传统方法依赖原始熵或批量统计无法提供稳定的反馈信号。

Method: SEGA对每个测试样本进行少量结构化擦除，观测预测熵随信息移除的变化轨迹，利用逐样本的敏感性轨迹来协调模型恢复和样本选择，而非依赖原始熵或批量统计。

Result: 在ImageNet-C、CIFAR10/100-C以及水产养殖腐蚀流数据上，SEGA相比强CTTA基线在鲁棒性和稳定性上均取得一致提升，并通过敏感性门控减少了反向传播次数。

Conclusion: 敏感性引导的擦除策略是一种无需周期重置或模型库的实用反馈机制，能够在长时程batch size为1的持续测试时适应中实现稳定的鲁棒性提升。

Abstract: Test-time adaptation (TTA) promises robustness under distribution shift by updating a pretrained model on unlabeled test data, but strict online TTA with batch size one and no access to source data is especially prone to drift or collapse. We introduce Sensitivity-Guided Erasing Adaptation (SEGA), a method for strict online continual TTA (CTTA) on corruption-style streams. SEGA uses a small number of structured erasures to probe how predictive entropy changes as information is removed, and uses the resulting per-sample sensitivity trajectories to coordinate recovery and sample selection rather than relying on raw entropy or batch statistics. This yields a practical feedback signal for long-horizon batch-size-one adaptation without periodic resets or model reservoirs. In experiments on ImageNet-C, CIFAR10/100-C, and corruption-generated aquaculture streams treated as controlled corruption-style proxies, SEGA yields consistent robustness and stability gains over strong CTTA baselines while reducing backward passes through sensitivity-based gating.

</details>


### [149] [Towards Continual Test-Time Adaptation of Vision-Language Models in Open-Vocabulary Semantic Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29923)
*Chandler Timm C. Doloriel,Yunbei Zhang,Sarthak Kumar Maharana,Muhammad Salman Siddiqui,Tor Kristian Stevik,Fadi Al Machot,Kristian Hovde Liland,Habib Ullah*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 针对开放词汇语义分割在持续测试时分布偏移下的脆弱性，提出DAF（Diversify, Anchor, Filter）稳定化框架，通过边际多样性损失、跨模态锚一致性损失和特征显著性过滤，在五个数据集上显著提升mIoU并保持稳定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开放词汇语义分割依赖视觉-语言对齐来识别任意文本定义的类别，但在持续的测试时分布偏移下，这种对齐变得脆弱。诊断分析发现三个问题：熵最小化导致斑块级类别坍缩、持续更新侵蚀视觉-语言对齐、低偏移样本的冗余梯度浪费计算资源。

Method: 提出DAF框架，包含三个核心组件：1）边际多样性损失（marginal diversity loss）抵抗类别坍缩；2）跨模态锚一致性损失（cross-modal anchor consistency loss）约束特征漂移，使其相对于冻结的源模型保持稳定；3）特征显著性过滤（feature salience filtering）跳过低价值的反向传播，以抵消源锚定带来的部分计算开销。

Result: 在五个覆盖自然场景、自动驾驶、水下影像和遥感的数据集（含损坏变体）上评估。DAF在熵最小化会崩溃的场景下保持稳定，相比源模型在Pascal VOC20-C上mIoU提升超过8个点，在LoveDA上提升超过9个点，在Foggy Cityscapes上提升超过3个点，并且对激进的适应策略和学习率选择具有鲁棒性。

Conclusion: DAF框架通过多头策略有效解决了开放词汇语义分割在持续测试时分布偏移下的稳定性问题，既提升了分割精度又保持了模型稳定性，且在多种设定下均表现鲁棒。

Abstract: Open-vocabulary semantic segmentation (OVSS) relies on vision-language alignment to recognize arbitrary text-defined categories, yet this alignment is fragile under continual test-time distribution shift. Our diagnostic analysis reveals that entropy minimization drives patch-level class collapse, continual updates erode vision-language alignment, and redundant gradients from low-shift samples waste computation. We propose Diversify, Anchor, and Filter (DAF), a stabilization framework that augments entropy-based adaptation with a marginal diversity loss that resists collapse, a cross-modal anchor consistency loss that constrains feature drift relative to a frozen source model, and feature salience filtering that skips low-value backward passes to offset part of the source-anchor overhead. We evaluate on five datasets spanning natural scenes, autonomous driving, underwater imagery, and remote sensing with their corrupted variants. Across the evaluated continual shifts, DAF remains stable where entropy minimization collapses, improving mIoU by over 8 points on Pascal VOC20-C, over 9 points on LoveDA, and over 3 points on Foggy Cityscapes compared to the source model, and is robust to aggressive adaptation and learning rate choices.

</details>


### [150] [Hallucination Mitigation for Large Vision-Language Models via Implicit Feature Stabilization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29924)
*Aditi Sarker,Rafi Ibn Sultan,Hui Zhu,Dongxiao Zhu,Prashant Khanduri*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出INFUSE框架，通过在微调阶段将扰动不变性内建于模型权重中，实现大规模视觉语言模型（LVLMs）的隐式去幻觉稳定化。在LLaVA-1.5、LLaVA-1.6和Qwen3-VL-8B-Instruct上，AMBER/CHAIR指标相对降低46-63%，同时保持VQA任务性能且无推理时开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LVLMs存在幻觉问题，作者发现部分幻觉源于表征的不稳定性——对输入进行轻微语义保持扰动会导致嵌入发生大的变化，且幻觉率与这种变异性呈正相关。现有基于稳定性的解决方案都是显式的，在推理时通过潜在导向或约束解码进行干预，每次查询都需要额外代价。

Method: 提出INFUSE框架，分两步：首先围绕扰动平均锚点和真实标签锚点稳定化视觉与文本表征，然后通过双向对比目标对齐跨模态的稳定化表征。理论上证明锚点与扰动均值表征的均方根偏差以1/√K的速率随视图数K收缩，并在Lipschitz解码器条件下，这一偏差能限制扰动对幻觉行为的影响。

Result: 在LLaVA-1.5、LLaVA-1.6和Qwen3-VL-8B-Instruct上，AMBER和CHAIR相对各基础模型降低46-63%；ObjHal、MMHal、HallusionBench和POPE均获得改善；VQA-v2和TextVQA性能保持不变；且无推理时开销。

Conclusion: 隐式稳定化是一种有效的LVLM幻觉缓解方案，无需推理时干预即可获得大幅度的幻觉率降低，同时保持下游任务性能，证明了将扰动不变性内建到模型权重中的可行性和优越性。

Abstract: Large Vision-Language Models (LVLMs) are prone to hallucinations: they fluently describe objects, attributes, and scenes that are not in the image. We connect part of this failure to a measurable property of their representations, feature instability, where mild semantics-preserving perturbations of the input cause large changes in the learned embeddings; hallucination rates rise together with this variability. Existing stability-motivated remedies are explicit, in the sense that they intervene at inference time through latent steering or constrained decoding, and pay for it on every query. We propose implicit stabilization instead: perturbation-invariance is built into the model weights during fine-tuning, and nothing extra runs at deployment. Our framework, INFUSE, first stabilizes visual and textual representations around perturbation-averaged and ground-truth anchors, then aligns the stabilized representations across modalities with bidirectional contrastive objectives. We prove that the anchor's root-mean-square deviation from the perturbation-mean representation shrinks at rate $1/\sqrt{K}$ in the number of views, and that under a Lipschitz decoder, this bounds how much any perturbation can change the model's hallucination behavior. On LLaVA-1.5, LLaVA-1.6, and Qwen3-VL-8B-Instruct, INFUSE reduces AMBER CHAIR by 46-63% relative to each base model, improves ObjHal, MMHal, HallusionBench, and POPE, and preserves VQA-v2 and TextVQA, all with no inference-time overhead.

</details>


### [151] [Dior: Drawing the Light of Image via Material-Decoupled Illumination Representation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29925)
*Xuanpu Zhang,Xuesong Niu,Haoxiang Cao,Ruidong Chen,Jianhao Zeng,Changqian Yu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Dior-Light方法，通过引入材料解耦的照明表示（Lumi Map），在手绘涂鸦与重光照结果之间建立显式映射，从而实现更准确和可控的图像重光照。方法通过渲染器合成三元组数据训练模型，并结合真实重光照对进行重建训练以缓解领域差距，在真实图像上实现了对光照强度和色度的有效控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法未能在手绘涂鸦输入和重光照结果之间建立一致且有效的映射，限制了对光照强度、色度及复杂空间分布的控制能力。

Method: 1) 提出材料解耦的Lumi Map表示，建立用户涂鸦与光照结果间显式映射；2) 使用渲染器合成"源图像-Lumi Map-重光照图像"三元组并训练模型；3) 在真实重光照对上执行重建训练以缓解合成数据带来的领域差距。

Result: 大量实验表明Dior-Light在重光照精度上优于现有方法，并能在真实世界图像上有效控制光照强度和色度。

Conclusion: 通过Lumi Map这一显式的材料解耦照明表示，成功解决了手绘涂鸦驱动的图像重光照中映射不一致的问题，显著提升了可控性和准确性。

Abstract: Controllable image relighting is an important problem in image editing, and hand-drawn scribbles provide an intuitive interface for specifying the desired illumination. However, existing methods do not establish a consistent and effective mapping between scribble inputs and relighting results, limiting their ability to control illumination intensity, chromaticity, and complex spatial distributions. We address this limitation by introducing a material-decoupled illumination representation, termed the Lumi Map, which establishes an explicit mapping between user scribbles and the resulting illumination, thereby improving both relighting accuracy and controllability. Specifically, we use a renderer to synthesize source image-Lumi Map-relit image triplets and train the model to predict the target relighting result conditioned on the Lumi Map. To mitigate the domain gap introduced by synthetic data, we further perform reconstruction training on real relighting pairs, improving the model's generalization to real-world images. Finally, we present Dior-Light, an image relighting method controlled by hand-drawn strokes. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our method outperforms existing approaches in relighting accuracy and enables effective control over illumination intensity and chromaticity on in-the-wild images.

</details>


### [152] [Everybody Tracking Every Body](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29927)
*Daeyun Shin,Yunhan Zhao,Shu Kong,Alexander C. Berg,Charless Fowlkes*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于扩散模型的融合方法，用于从多人的第一人称视角视频和IMU数据中估计3D人体姿态，通过融合基于头部运动的自中心姿态估计与基于视频的旁观者姿态观测，同时考虑观测内容的可靠性和质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多人交互场景下的3D人体姿态估计具有挑战性：每个个体的第一人称视角只能间歇性地、不可靠地观察到其他人，且不同相机和主体的运动导致观测可靠性高度变化。现有方法难以有效整合这些异构、稀疏且质量不稳定的多源数据。

Method: 提出一种扩散模型（diffusion-based）方法，将来自自中心相机运动（经VIO SLAM处理的头部运动）推导的姿态估计，与来自旁观者视角的异中心姿态观测进行融合。模型以观测内容和观测可靠性为条件进行训练，训练数据混合了单人动作捕捉数据和多人视频数据，以学习丰富的身体运动轨迹先验和视频观测可靠性模型。

Result: 在具有挑战性的多人数据集上的评估表明，所提出的融合方法在绝对姿态精度和相对姿态精度方面均优于仅基于运动信息（motion-only）和仅基于视觉信息（vision-only）的基线方法。

Conclusion: 扩散模型能够有效地融合自中心运动估计和异中心视觉观测，利用集中式协调将多个第一人称视角的数据流整合，显著提升了多人交互场景中3D姿态估计的准确性。

Abstract: We address the problem of 3D body pose estimation of multiple interacting people from their egocentric views with centralized coordination. Each individual wears a camera recording egocentric video and IMU data. Processing this video with VIO SLAM provides high-quality tracking of each egocentric camera through space. The first-person view from one individual provides third-person observations of other people, although these exocentric observations are sparse, intermittent, and of highly variable reliability as both cameras and subjects move. To integrate these synchronized data streams, we propose a diffusion-based approach that fuses estimates of pose based on head motion derived from egocentric camera motion with exocentric pose observations, conditioning on both observation content and reliability. Our model is trained on a mixture of single-person motion-capture data and multi-person video in order to learn rich priors for body motion trajectories and video observation reliability. Evaluation on challenging multi-person datasets suggests our fusion approach improves over motion-only and vision-only baselines in terms of both absolute and relative pose accuracy.

</details>


### [153] [Biomechanical 3D Body: Self-Supervised Distillation of Biomechanical Pose from a 3D Body Foundation Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29928)
*R. James Cotton,J. D. Peiffer,Lucinda Williamson,John Leske,Georgios Pavlakos*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出在SAM-3D-Body基础模型上扩展一个生物力学预测头，从单张RGB图像回归生物力学模型的关节角度和尺度。通过Levenberg-Marquardt求解器进行逆运动学拟合生成优化目标来蒸馏训练，并在JAX/Equinox中实现与MuJoCo GPU优化模型兼容。在MoVi、BioCV和临床队列数据上验证，性能优于现有的图像直接回归生物力学方法，仅略逊于需要整个轨迹推理优化的最先进方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有单目人体恢复方法输出网格顶点和运动树角度，但缺乏生物力学定义的关节角度，无法满足临床和生物力学分析等下游应用的需求。

Method: 在SAM-3D-Body基础模型上增加生物力学预测头；利用Levenberg-Marquardt求解器对网格预测的标记点进行逆运动学拟合，生成优化目标用于蒸馏训练；使用JAX和Equinox实现以兼容MuJoCo中的GPU优化生物力学模型。

Result: 在MoVi、BioCV和临床队列数据集上验证，模型优于现有的图像直接回归生物力学方法，仅略逊于执行更昂贵的全轨迹推理优化的最先进单目生物力学方法。

Conclusion: 通过知识蒸馏策略，即使缺乏成对的图像-生物力学数据，也能从网格预测中有效蒸馏出生物力学预测头，为临床和生物力学分析提供所需的关节角度输出。

Abstract: State-of-the-art monocular body recovery methods predict mesh vertices and angles on the corresponding kinematic tree, but their outputs lack biomechanically defined joint angles that downstream applications like clinical and biomechanical analyses require. We extend an existing foundation model, SAM-3D-Body, with an additional biomechanical prediction head that, from a single RGB image, regresses the joint angles and scales of a biomechanical model. Training this model presents a challenge, as there are limited datasets of paired images and biomechanical fits. To overcome this, we supervise biomechanical outputs with in-loop optimized targets from a Levenberg-Marquardt solver performing inverse kinematics fits against markers from the mesh predictions. This allows distilling the biomechanical head from the mesh head, even from unlabeled images. To make this work with GPU-optimized biomechanical models in MuJoCo, the entire model was implemented in JAX using Equinox. We trained this distilled output head on the publicly released SAM-3D-Body dataset. We then validated this model on biomechanical fits to two publicly available marker-based datasets, MoVi and BioCV, as well as movements from a clinical cohort captured with multiview markerless motion capture. The resulting model outperforms existing models for direct regression of biomechanics from images while only slightly underperforming the state-of-the-art monocular biomechanics method that performs more costly inference-time optimization of entire trajectories.

</details>


### [154] [Evaluating 2D and 3D-Aware Vision Foundation Models for Vehicle Attribute Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29929)
*Alexandre V. Delazeri,Gabriel E. Lima,Eduil Nascimento,Rayson Laroca,David Menotti*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文对14种先进的2D和3D感知视觉基础模型进行了实证基准测试，在UFPR-VeSV真实世界数据集上评估其车辆属性识别（类型、品牌、型号）能力。研究发现标准2D自监督模型（尤其是DINOv3）在细粒度任务上大幅优于3D感知模型（宏平均准确率超93%），但3D感知模型Depth Anything v2在车型分类中对视角变化表现出更强的鲁棒性，因此建议结合2D和3D先验进行混合建模。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在车牌识别不可用或不可靠时，车辆属性识别对智能交通系统至关重要。然而视觉基础模型在细粒度车辆分类上的有效性尚未被充分探索，特别是3D感知基础模型相对于传统2D架构是否具有优势仍不明确，因此需要系统性的实证评估。

Method: 对14种最新的2D和3D感知视觉基础模型进行实证基准测试，使用UFPR-VeSV数据集，将模型作为冻结特征提取器，通过线性探测进行车辆类型、品牌和型号识别；并对最佳模型进一步进行小样本学习和分布外域偏移压力测试。

Result: 标准2D自监督模型尤其是DINOv3在细粒度任务上显著优于3D感知模型，品牌和型号识别的宏平均准确率超过93%；而3D感知的Depth Anything v2在车型分类中对视角变化具有更强的不变性。

Conclusion: 2D基础模型目前仍是细粒度车辆识别的主流选择，但3D感知模型在视角鲁棒性方面具有独特优势，未来应探索结合2D和3D先验的混合方法，以实现更稳健的车辆识别性能。

Abstract: Vehicle attribute recognition is an important task in intelligent transportation systems, particularly when Automatic License Plate Recognition (ALPR) is unavailable or unreliable. Although vision foundation models have shown strong transferability across domains, their effectiveness for fine-grained vehicle classification remains underexplored. Moreover, given the inherently three-dimensional structure of vehicles, it is unclear whether emerging 3D-aware foundation models offer advantages over standard 2D architectures. This paper presents an empirical benchmark of 14 state-of-the-art 2D and 3D-aware vision foundation models. Using the challenging real-world UFPR-VeSV dataset, we evaluate these models as frozen feature extractors via linear probing for vehicle type, make, and model recognition. We further stress-test the best-performing models under few-shot learning and Out-of-Distribution (OOD) domain shifts. Our results show that standard 2D self-supervised models, particularly DINOv3, substantially outperform 3D-aware models in fine-grained tasks, achieving over 93% Macro-Accuracy for make and model recognition. However, the 3D-aware Depth Anything v2 exhibits stronger invariance to viewing angles in vehicle type classification. These findings motivate hybrid approaches that combine 2D and 3D priors for robust vehicle recognition. Our code is publicly available at https://github.com/UFPR-IPASPPR/3D-Vision-Benchmark/.

</details>


### [155] [On the Role of MRI Sequences in Cross-Dataset Generalization for Brain Tumor Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29944)
*Henrique Zan Grande,João G. Pitol,Lucas B. Schuck,Rafael V. Serenato,Rayson Laroca,Andre Gustavo Hochuli*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文系统评估了MRI不同序列（模态）对脑肿瘤分割模型跨数据集鲁棒性的影响，发现T2f/FLAIR序列在跨数据集性能上表现最佳（Dice>75%），多序列训练能进一步提升性能，且有限的域适应即可带来显著的性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管U-Net等深度学习架构在脑肿瘤分割中取得成功，但跨数据集性能退化仍是重大挑战，尤其是在域偏移和标注数据有限的情况下。现有研究缺乏对单一MRI序列影响模型鲁棒性的系统性评估。

Method: 采用ResUNet框架，将每个MRI模态独立训练以隔离其影响，在控制肿瘤大小分层的跨数据集评估协议下进行实验，设置无目标域训练或有限域适应的条件，系统对比不同序列的跨数据集性能。

Result: T2f/FLAIR序列在跨数据集分割中表现最佳，Dice得分超过75%，且在大多数肿瘤尺寸范围内持续优于其他模态；多序列训练能进一步提升性能；即使有限的域适应也能带来快速的初期性能提升。

Conclusion: T2f/FLAIR是跨数据集脑肿瘤分割中最具鲁棒性的单一模态，多模态训练和有限的域适应可以有效缓解域偏移问题，降低对大量标注数据和昂贵重训练的需求。

Abstract: Brain tumor segmentation in magnetic resonance imaging (MRI) is a critical task for diagnosis and treatment planning. Despite the success of deep learning architectures such as U-Net and its variants, performance degradation across datasets remains a major challenge, particularly under domain shift and limited annotated data. To address this issue, this study systematically evaluates how individual MRI sequences influence model robustness across two well-known datasets. A ResUNet-based framework is employed, where each modality is trained independently to isolate its effect under a controlled cross-dataset evaluation protocol with tumor size stratification, without target-domain training, or with limited domain adaptation. Results show that the T2f/FLAIR sequence achieves the best cross-dataset performance, with Dice scores exceeding 75%. It consistently outperforms other modalities across most tumor size ranges, while multi-sequence training further improves performance. Additionally, even limited target-domain adaptation yields rapid initial gains, reducing the need for extensive annotations and costly retraining. Our source code is publicly available at https://github.com/henrique-zan/brain_tumor_segmentation/.

</details>


### [156] [RIDGE: Region-Informed Derivative-Guided Evidence Selection for Long Video Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29958)
*Shanqing Xu,Meng Luo,Mengchen Qian,Yuhui Gao,Siyue Peng,Xiaohan Zhong,Xiaojin Zhang,Zhongyu Wei,Wei Chen,Xiang Bai*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出RIDGE，一种将帧-查询相似度曲线视为时序信号进行结构分析的帧选择框架，通过局部变化和曲率将时间线划分为结构区域，并按区域挑选帧以保留事件核心、过渡、铺垫、余波和上下文帧，无需训练且计算轻量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有查询感知的帧选择方法通常将相似度序列仅视为排序或采样的数值，忽略了其作为有序信号所反映的查询相关证据随时间出现、达到峰值和消退的形状信息。这导致许多解释、铺垫或延续事件的帧（位于峰值两侧）因绝对分数较低而被遗漏。

Method: RIDGE将帧-查询相似度曲线作为时间信号读取，利用局部变化和曲率将时间线划分为结构区域（如事件核心、过渡、铺垫、余波等），然后应用区域特定的选择策略，在固定预算下保留不同类型的重要帧。该方法作为预计算帧-查询分数的轻量后处理步骤，无需训练或迭代调用LVLM。

Result: 在四个长视频基准和三个骨干模型上，RIDGE在大多数设置下取得了最佳性能，在其他设置中保持了竞争力。

Conclusion: 将相似度序列视为时间信号而非单纯数值可显著改善长视频的帧选择质量，RIDGE以无训练、轻量的方式有效保留了查询相关证据的结构性信息。

Abstract: Long videos contain far more visual content than Large Vision-Language Models (LVLMs) can process under a fixed visual-token budget, making frame selection essential. Existing query-aware selectors usually estimate frame-query relevance and build a compact subset from high-scoring frames. Although their mechanisms differ, the similarity sequence is still often treated primarily as values to rank or sample from, rather than as an ordered signal whose shape reflects how query-relevant evidence emerges, peaks, and fades over time. This can obscure frames that explain, contextualize, or follow an event, because such evidence may lie on the rising or falling sides of a nearby relevance peak and receive lower absolute scores. We propose RIDGE, a frame selection framework that reads the frame-query similarity curve as a temporal signal. By using local changes and curvature, RIDGE partitions the timeline into structural regions and applies region-specific selection to preserve event cores, transitions, buildup, aftermath, and contextual frames under a fixed budget. It is a lightweight post-processing step on precomputed frame-query scores and requires neither training nor iterative LVLM calls. Across four long-video benchmarks and three backbones, RIDGE achieves the best performance in most settings and remains competitive in the others.

</details>


### [157] [Confidence-Aware Ensemble and Long-Word Refinement for Artistic Text Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29970)
*Lucas A. Dias,Henrique A. Schulz,Rafaela de Miranda,Guilherme L. Peres,Pedro L. Bittencourt,Rayson Laroca*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文在WordArt-V1.5基准上提出一种置信度感知的集成方法，结合SVTRv2、PARSeq和MAERec三种识别器，并引入基于Needleman-Wunsch比对和词典纠错的长词细化阶段，最终在Test B上达到89.90%的识别准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 艺术文本识别具有挑战性，因为单词图像常包含装饰性字体、弯曲布局、类似物体的字符、杂乱背景和严重畸变，现有识别器在该场景下性能有限。

Method: 提出置信度感知的集成方法：1) 集成SVTRv2、PARSeq和MAERec三个在官方训练集上微调的模型；2) 在预测不一致的位置上取最小置信度来选择最终预测；3) 针对长词引入基于Needleman-Wunsch对齐和词典引导纠正的细化阶段。

Result: 在WordArt-V1.5 Test B上达到89.90%的单词识别准确率，比最佳单模型提升1.77个百分点；长词细化使目标长词子集提升2.72个百分点；错误分析显示48.8%的剩余错误与标注问题、视觉模糊或不可读样本相关。

Conclusion: 置信度感知集成方法有效提升艺术文本识别性能，同时错误诊断报告揭示了基准中近半数错误源于数据本身质量而非模型缺陷，强调了诊断性报告对未来ATR基准和模型发展的重要性。源码已开源。

Abstract: Artistic Text Recognition (ATR) remains challenging because word images often combine decorative fonts, curved layouts, object-like characters, clutter, and severe distortions. This paper studies WordArt-V1.5 as a standardized benchmark for this setting and evaluates recent scene and artistic text recognizers under a common protocol. We propose a confidence-aware ensemble that combines SVTRv2, PARSeq, and MAERec after fine-tuning on the official training split. The ensemble selects predictions using the minimum confidence over disagreement positions, emphasizing characters that separate competing hypotheses. For long words, where a single character error can invalidate the whole prediction, we add a targeted refinement stage based on Needleman-Wunsch alignment and lexicon-guided correction. On the WordArt-V1.5 Test B split, the proposed system reaches 89.90% Word Recognition Accuracy, improving the best individual fine-tuned model by 1.77 percentage points. The long-word refinement produces a modest global gain, but improves the targeted long-word subset by 2.72 percentage points. Finally, an error analysis of all remaining mistakes shows that 48.8% are associated with labeling issues, visual ambiguity, or illegible samples, highlighting the value of diagnostic reporting for future ATR benchmarks and models. Our source code is available at https://github.com/lucas-azdias/Artistic-Text-Recognition/.

</details>


### [158] [SpanCalib-VLM: Calibrated Hallucination Span Detection in Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29974)
*Amanuel Gizachew Abebe,Yasmin Moslem*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出SpanCalib-VLM，一种针对大型视觉语言模型幻觉检测的混合双系统方法，结合多模态序列标注器（XLM-RoBERTa-Large + SigLIP）与微调生成式VLM（Qwen3.5-4B），通过Union-Calibrated Fusion策略实现对幻觉span的精准定位和置信度校准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型视觉语言模型（LVLMs）的幻觉检测需要同时满足准确的span定位和良好的置信度校准。微调生成式VLM虽然能准确识别幻觉span但存在过度自信和推理延迟高的问题；判别式序列标注器虽然速度快且校准好但span召回率保守。两种方法的优势互补是解决该问题的关键。

Method: 提出SpanCalib-VLM混合双系统架构：(1) 多模态序列标注器，由XLM-RoBERTa-Large与SigLIP视觉编码器通过交叉注意力融合；(2) 微调的生成式VLM（Qwen3.5-4B-SHROOM-SFT）。通过Union-Calibrated Fusion策略，将生成模型产出的候选span用序列标注器的校准概率重新打分。

Result: 在SHROOM-Visions英文评估集上，Pearson校准相关系数达0.41，总体IoU为0.39，干净响应IoU高达0.91，总体检测准确率为70.7%。

Conclusion: 混合双系统架构有效融合了生成式模型的span定位能力与判别式标注器的校准优势，在幻觉检测任务上取得了优于单一方法的性能，且代码和模型权重已公开。

Abstract: Detecting hallucinations in Large Vision-Language Models (LVLMs) requires both accurate span localization and well-calibrated confidence scores. Fine-tuned generative VLMs excel at identifying hallucinated text spans but suffer from overconfidence and high inference latency. Discriminative sequence taggers offer deterministic speed and superior calibration but exhibit conservative span recall. We present SpanCalib-VLM, a hybrid dual-system for the SHROOM-Visions Shared Task that combines a multimodal sequence tagger, consisting of XLM-RoBERTa-Large fused with a SigLIP vision encoder via cross-attention, with our fine-tuned generative VLM (Qwen3.5-4B-SHROOM-SFT). Through a Union-Calibrated Fusion strategy, candidate spans from the generative model are re-scored with calibrated probabilities from the sequence tagger. On the SHROOM-Visions English evaluation split, our ensemble achieves a Pearson calibration correlation of 0.41 and an overall IoU of 0.39, with a clean-response IoU of 0.91} and overall detection accuracy of 70.7%. We make our model weights and code publicly available.

</details>


### [159] [FIS-OT: Feature-Induced Optimal Transport for Unsupervised Action Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29980)
*Linxiang Peng,Xinyao Qin,Jinhan Li,Di Yang,Jiangtao Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出FIS-OT框架，一种基于特征诱导的结构化最优传输方法，用于无监督动作分割任务。通过特征增强生成器（FEG）模块利用三元组损失捕获局部一致性，并引入特征诱导残差结构先验结合固定时间骨架与动态特征相似性，最后通过循环优化循环对齐局部特征学习与全局结构对齐，在三个数据集上验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有最优传输（OT）方法使用全局约束，忽略了局部信息的利用；同时现有多数OT架构过度信任自身生成的伪标签，导致早期训练阶段从噪声中学习，产生确认偏差问题。

Method: 提出FIS-OT框架，包含三个核心组件：1）特征增强生成器（FEG）模块作为内部正则器，通过三元组损失捕获局部一致性，提供独立于噪声伪标签的稳健监督；2）特征诱导残差结构先验，结合固定时间骨架与动态特征相似性，保证时间连续性并适应复杂动作结构；3）循环优化循环，对齐局部特征学习与全局结构对齐。

Result: 在三个数据集上的大量实验验证了所提出方法的有效性，表明FIS-OT在无监督动作分割任务上取得了良好的性能表现。

Conclusion: FIS-OT通过引入特征增强生成器和特征诱导残差结构先验，有效解决了现有OT方法忽略局部信息以及确认偏差的问题，为无监督动作分割提供了一种新的有效解决方案。

Abstract: Unsupervised action segmentation is a challenging task. It involves finding action categories and boundaries in videos without labels. Existing Optimal Transport (OT) methods use global constraints. This causes them to overlook the use of local information. Furthermore, existing Optimal transport architectures are prone to confirmation bias because they overly trust the pseudo-labels they generate. This causes models to learn from noise in the early training stages. To address these issues, we propose FIS-OT. It is a novel Feature-Induced Structured Optimal Transport framework. First, we introduce a Feature Enhanced Generator (FEG) module. It serves as an internal regularizer. By using triplet loss, FEG captures local consistency. It provides robust supervision that is independent of noisy pseudo-labels. Second, we propose a Feature-Induced Residual Structural Prior. This combines a fixed temporal backbone with dynamic feature similarities. This design ensures temporal continuity. It also allows the solver to adapt to complex action structures. Finally, we establish a cyclic optimization loop. This aligns local feature learning with global structural alignment. Extensive experiments on the three datasets show the effectiveness of our method.

</details>


### [160] [SVI2LoD3: Agent-Driven Reconstruction of LoD3 Facade Openings in Semantic 3D City Models from Volunteered Street View Imagery using Large Language and Visual Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29992)
*Elmehdi Kanna,Lukas Arzoumanidis,Huynh Duc An Son Nguyen,Youness Dehbi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种端到端的、基于智能体驱动的LoD3立面开口重建管线，采用零样本分割策略替代传统监督语义分割，无需大量人工标注数据，同时通过强制部分学层级关系生成符合CityGML标准的LoD3建筑模型，并引入新的评估指标Facade Feature Distance (FFD)来衡量立面重建质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LoD3立面重建方法依赖监督语义分割，需要大量人工标注的训练数据，成本高昂；同时传统评估指标如mIoU或FRDS仅通过像素级重叠度量相似性，无法充分捕捉语义正确性和建筑布局信息。

Method: 采用端到端的智能体驱动管道，核心包括：(1)零样本分割策略替代监督语义分割，大幅减少标注工作量；(2)强制部分学层级关系以确保CityGML符合规范；(3)提出基于视觉Transformer高维特征空间的Facade Feature Distance (FFD)评估指标，综合度量语义正确性与建筑布局。

Result: 在eTRIMS数据集上的基准测试中取得了强劲的性能表现，在减少人工标注成本的同时保持了较高的重建质量，并生成了可直接使用的CityGML标准LoD3输出。

Conclusion: 该管线与评估策略为自动化生成和分析语义丰富的3D城市模型提供了实用且可扩展的解决方案，代码已公开发布。

Abstract: This paper presents an end-to-end, agent-driven pipeline for the LoD3 reconstruction of facade openings in 3D city models, producing directly usable CityGML-conform outputs. In contrast to existing approaches that rely on supervised semantic segmentation and therefore require large amounts of manually annotated training data, the proposed method employs a zero-shot segmentation strategy. This substantially reduces the annotation effort while still achieving strong performance in our benchmark on the eTRIMS dataset. A further key contribution is the enforcement of correct partonomic hierarchies, thereby producing CityGML-conform LoD3 building models. Beyond the reconstruction pipeline itself, this work also introduces a novel evaluation metric for facade reconstruction, termed Facade Feature Distance (FFD). Unlike conventional metrics such as mIoU or FRDS, which assess similarity primarily through pixel-wise overlap, FFD measures distance in a high-level feature space derived from a vision transformer. In doing so, it captures both semantic correctness and architectural layout, providing a more suitable assessment of facade reconstruction quality. The proposed pipeline and evaluation strategy together offer a practical and scalable contribution toward the automated generation and analysis of semantically enriched 3D city models. The developed code is published at: https://github.com/hcu-cml/citydb-SVI2LoD3-ai.

</details>


### [161] [Partition-Aware Unlearning for Removing Spurious Correlations in Large Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29996)
*Aditi Sarker,Nazreen Shah,Rafi Ibn Sultan,Rhongho Jang,Dongxiao Zhu,Prashant Khanduri*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出PURGE框架，用于构建、基准测试和缓解大型视觉语言模型（LVLMs）中由虚假目标-背景相关性引发的预测错误，通过结构化数据集构建和分区感知反学习来减少幻觉并保持整体性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LVLMs常常利用虚假的对象-背景相关性做出预测，而非基于对象相关的视觉证据。现有基准缺乏对模型预测是否真正基于目标对象或由相关背景线索诱导的精细控制，亟需一个可诊断并缓解这类系统性失败的框架。

Method: PURGE框架包含两部分：(1) 结构化数据集构建，提出三种互补的数据构建策略，将样本按对象相关证据与虚假背景线索进行分区，实现捷径依赖的受控诊断；(2) 分区感知反学习，利用这些分区选择性地去除虚假的对象-背景关联，同时保留基于对象的推理能力。

Result: 在LLaVA-1.6-7B、Qwen3-VL-8B-Instruct、Qwen3.5-9B及CLIP等多种模型上，于CHAIR、POPE、Causal-HalBench、MM-SpuBench、AMBER、MMHal、Waterbirds等基准上进行评估，PURGE在大多数场景下持续减少幻觉和虚假相关性驱动的错误，同时维持或提升整体性能。

Conclusion: PURGE提供了一个可复用的评估协议和有效的缓解框架，有助于构建更可靠的LVLMs，兼顾幻觉抑制与性能保持。

Abstract: Large Vision-Language Models (LVLMs) achieve strong performance across many multimodal tasks; however, they often exploit spurious object-background correlations, resulting in predictions driven by contextual shortcuts rather than object-relevant visual evidence. Despite growing interest in hallucination and robustness evaluation, existing benchmarks provide limited control over whether model predictions are grounded in the target object or induced by correlated background cues. In this work, we introduce PURGE (\underline{P}artition-aware \underline{U}nlearning for \underline{R}emoving spurious-correlation \underline{G}enerated \underline{E}rrors), a framework for constructing, benchmarking, and mitigating spurious-correlation-induced failures in LVLMs. The framework consists of: -- (1) Structured dataset construction wherein we develop three complementary structured data construction strategies that partition examples by object-relevant evidence and spurious background cues, enabling controlled diagnosis of shortcut reliance; and -- (2) Partition-aware unlearning, which uses these partitions to selectively remove spurious object-background associations while preserving object-based reasoning. We evaluate the \algo~framework across multiple LVLMs, including LLaVA-1.6-7B, Qwen3-VL-8B-Instruct, and Qwen3.5-9B, together with CLIP as a vision-language encoder, on a diverse suite of benchmarks, including CHAIR, POPE, Causal-HalBench, MM-SpuBench, AMBER, MMHal, and Waterbirds. Our results show that PURGE consistently reduces hallucinations and spurious-correlation-driven errors while maintaining or improving overall performance in most evaluated settings, providing both a reusable evaluation protocol and an effective mitigation framework for more reliable LVLMs.

</details>


### [162] [Discrete Diffusion Bridges for Spatiotemporally Aligned Image Translation and Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29997)
*Xing Xie,Jiawei Liu,Shijun Zhou,Huijie Fan,Zhi Han,Yandong Tang,Liangqiong Qu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出离散扩散桥（DDB）框架，通过混合吸收机制和信息引导噪声调度，解决标准离散扩散在图像翻译与生成中的时空错位问题，在多种生成范式下实现高效、高质量的图像变换。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准离散扩散在图像翻译和生成中存在固有的时空错位问题：空间上，纯掩码状态完全丢弃源图像的结构先验；时间上，随机掩码顺序与推理时"先易后难"的解码机制相矛盾。这些问题限制了离散扩散在图像翻译任务中的表现。

Method: DDB构建了域间直接高效的轨迹。空间上引入混合吸收机制，将吸收状态重新定义为掩码与源token的随机混合，将源先验作为空间锚点注入潜在空间；时间上设计信息引导噪声调度，通过量化语义变化，优先在高信息区域的早期时间步进行破坏，使模型利用不变区域的稳健上下文解决困难的语义变化。

Result: 大量实验验证了DDB在多种生成范式中的通用性和鲁棒性。该方法在文本引导语义操作和纯结构图像翻译中有效平衡了编辑对齐与结构保真度，同时天然补充文本到图像生成，并在极低采样步数下保证稳健的高质量解码。

Conclusion: DDB通过时空两个维度的针对性设计，成功解决了离散扩散在图像翻译中的核心错位问题，为多模态生成任务提供了统一的解决方案，并已在GitHub上开源代码和模型。

Abstract: We propose Discrete Diffusion Bridges (DDB), a novel framework designed to resolve the fundamental spatiotemporal misalignment of standard discrete diffusion in image translation and generation. By corrupting data into a pure mask state via a random schedule, the conventional forward process induces a twofold misalignment: spatially, this pure-mask destination entirely discards the rich structural priors of the source image; temporally, the random masking order inherently contradicts the ``easy-first, hard-last'' decoding mechanism used during inference. To address this, DDB constructs a direct and efficient trajectory between domains. Spatially, we introduce a hybrid absorption mechanism that redefines the absorbing state to a stochastic mixture of mask and source tokens, effectively injecting source prior as spatial anchors into the latent space. Temporally, we design an information-guided noise schedule that quantifies semantic variation to prioritize the corruption of high-information regions at earlier timesteps. This ensures the model learns to resolve difficult semantic changes using robust context from invariant regions. Extensive experiments validate the versatility and robustness of our framework across diverse generative paradigms. DDB effectively balances edit alignment with structural fidelity across both text-guided semantic manipulation and pure structural image translation, while inherently complementing text-to-image generation and guaranteeing robust high-quality decoding under extremely low sampling steps. Code and models are available at \href{https://github.com/HKU-HealthAI/DDB}{https://github.com/HKU-HealthAI/DDB}.

</details>


### [163] [OPAL: Orthonormal Prototype Alignment Learning for Interpretable Image Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30003)
*Ilán Carretero,Gustavo Jesús Angulo,Rocío del Amor,Valery Naranjo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出OPAL，一种单阶段端到端的原型部件可解释分类框架，通过预定义正交基锚定潜在空间，并利用空间竞争机制实现精确的部件定位，无需辅助损失即可优于非可解释方法和其他原型方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的原型部件模型虽然能提供可解释的预测，但训练流程复杂、多阶段，且严重依赖辅助正则化来防止原型坍缩，限制了其实际应用。

Method: OPAL采用单阶段端到端框架，使用预定义正交基锚定潜在空间，将每个类别嵌入由固定部件原型张成的专用子空间中。通过特征图上的空间竞争机制实现精确的部件定位，隔离稀疏的判别区域，使每个原型在不同图像中一致地关注相同的语义概念。分类被重构为直接的表示对齐任务，无需辅助损失。

Result: 在细粒度基准数据集上的大量实验表明，OPAL在性能上超越了非可解释的对应方法以及最先进的原型部件方法，并能通过显式揭示每个预测所依据的具体图像区域提供细粒度的视觉解释。

Conclusion: OPAL通过消除多阶段训练和辅助正则化的需求，简化了可解释分类流程，同时提升了性能与可解释性，为原型部件模型提供了更高效、更简洁的解决方案。

Abstract: Prototypical part-based models provide explainable predictions by comparing input regions to learned prototypes. However, current approaches are burdened by complex, multi-stage training pipelines and heavily rely on auxiliary regularization to prevent prototype collapse. To overcome these limitations, we introduce Orthonormal Prototype Alignment Learning (OPAL), a single-stage, end-to-end framework that simplifies interpretable classification. Our approach anchors the latent space using predefined orthonormal bases, embedding each class within a dedicated subspace spanned by fixed part-prototypes. To achieve precise part localization, OPAL enforces spatial competition across feature maps. This mechanism isolates sparse, discriminative regions, directing each prototype to consistently attend to the same semantic concept across different images. By framing classification as a direct representation alignment task, our method eliminates the need for auxiliary losses. Extensive experiments on fine-grained benchmarks demonstrate that OPAL outperforms both its non-interpretable counterparts and state-of-the-art part-prototype methods, delivering granular visual explanations by explicitly revealing the specific image regions driving every prediction. Code is available at https://github.com/ilancarretero/OPAL.

</details>


### [164] [Input-Adaptive Gating of a Dehazing Front-End for On-Device Perception in Smoke-Obscured Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30034)
*Seongjun Kang,Ishaan Garg,Vishnu Bharadwaj*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文评估了消防辅助管道中"先增强后任务"的两阶段视觉管线，发现雾霾去除在浓烟下有助于边缘检测，但在清晰或轻雾帧上反而有害，因此提出基于暗通道雾霾估计的动态门控机制，仅在有需要时运行去雾网络，在Raspberry Pi 4上既提升精度又大幅节省计算时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 两阶段视觉管线通常假设"输入越干净，输出越好"，即增强网络置于任务网络之前。但这一假设未得到充分验证，尤其在边缘检测这类对细节敏感的任务中，去雾可能丢弃有效信息反而降低性能。作者希望在消防员辅助场景中系统评估这一假设。

Method: 构建轻量化去雾网络（355K参数）与边缘检测网络（23K参数），均量化为UINT8 TensorFlow Lite部署在树莓派4上；通过暗通道雾霾估计设计动态门控，仅当雾霾估计超过阈值时运行去雾；对比有/无去雾及门控策略在四个雾霾等级下的边缘检测精度（F-measure ODS）和推理时间。

Result: 去雾网络在真实浓烟雾上达到18.60 dB PSNR（原始13.60 dB，AOD-Net 17.08 dB）；边缘检测器ODS 0.738优于优化Canny的0.692；去雾在浓烟下提升边缘提取但在清晰/轻雾帧上降低性能；门控机制平均ODS 0.675优于始终去雾0.664和从不去雾0.630，且将平均帧时间从569ms降至321ms，清晰帧帧率提升五倍（1.8到9 fps）。

Conclusion: "先增强后任务"的假设并非普遍成立：去雾在浓雾下有益但在轻雾/清晰场景下有害。动态门控根据雾霾强度有条件地执行增强，是兼顾精度和效率的更优策略，证明在资源受限的边缘设备上当有选择地使用预处理网络。

Abstract: Two-stage vision pipelines often place an enhancement network before a task network, on the assumption that a cleaner input produces a better output. We evaluate this in a firefighter assistance pipeline, where a dehazer precedes an edge detector that renders smoke-filled rooms as structural outlines. Both were designed for a Raspberry Pi 4, at 355K and 23K parameters, and quantized to UINT8 via TensorFlow Lite. The float dehazer reaches 18.60 dB peak signal-to-noise ratio (PSNR) on held-out real smoke against 13.60 dB unprocessed and 17.08 dB for an AOD-Net trained on the same data, and the edge detector reaches an F-measure at optimal dataset scale (ODS) of 0.738, outperforming an optimized Canny's result of 0.692. Dehazing improves edge extraction under dense smoke but degrades it on clear and lightly hazed frames, where the dehazer discards more detail than the haze obscures. We therefore run the dehazer only when a dark channel haze estimate exceeds a threshold, a 10.1 ms test that lets the pipeline save 469.6 ms on the dehazing stage. Averaged over four haze levels, gating is more accurate than either fixed decision, at 0.675 mean ODS against 0.664 for always dehazing and 0.630 for never dehazing. It reduces the mean per-frame time on the Raspberry Pi from 569 ms to 321 ms, and on clear frames increases the frame rate fivefold, from 1.8 to 9 frames per second.

</details>


### [165] [TAKE 85: Testing Audiovisual filmmaKer's intEnt across 85 Hours of Film](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30068)
*Kaishuu Shinozaki-Conefrey,Olivier Pascaud,Robin Courant,Xi Wang,Dimitris Samaras,Vicky Kalogeiton*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出TAKE 85基准，首个用于导演意图理解的多模态基准，包含398部短片和专家验证的问答对，实验表明现有MLLMs在意图理解上表现远低于感知识别。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态大语言模型(MLLMs)的评估几乎全部聚焦于理解"发生了什么"（事件与情节），而忽略了"为什么这样呈现"（导演意图）这一影视叙事中的核心维度。人类能够自然地解读光线、色彩、构图、剪辑等创作选择背后的导演意图，但当前模型在此方面尚未被系统性评估。

Method: 构建TAKE 85基准，包含398部短片（共85小时）和专家验证的问答对，覆盖全局与细粒度的视觉和音频意图。通过受控模态消融实验，系统评估多模态推理能力。

Result: 实验发现感知识别与意图理解之间存在巨大差距：模型能准确描述事件和叙事，但无法推断电影制作决策的沟通作用。最强模型仅得58/100分，且消融实验表明没有任何单一输入模态足以独立完成意图理解。

Conclusion: 导演意图是此前被忽视的多模态理解维度；现有最强的MLLMs在此方面表现显著不足，需要新的研究方向。所有代码、问答对和模型均已公开。

Abstract: Films communicate through deliberate creative choices, including lighting, color, composition, editing, dialogue, music, and sound. Humans naturally interpret these signals as directorial intent, yet current multimodal large language models (MLLMs) are evaluated almost exclusively on understanding what happens rather than why it is presented that way. We introduce TAKE 85, the first benchmark for directorial-intent understanding, comprising 398 short films (85 hours) with expert-verified question-answer pairs spanning global and fine-grained visual and audio intent. Through controlled modality ablations, TAKE 85 enables systematic evaluation of multimodal reasoning. Experiments on state-of-the-art MLLMs reveal a substantial gap between perceptual recognition and intentional understanding: while models accurately describe events and narratives, they consistently fail to infer the communicative role of filmmaking decisions. Our results establish directorial intent as a previously overlooked dimension of multimodal understanding: even the strongest model reaches only 58 out of 100, and our ablations show that no input modality is sufficient on its own. All code, Q&As, and models are publicly available from https://github.com/KaiShinozakiConefrey/Take-85

</details>


### [166] [The Nearest Target Is the Wrong One: Target Separation in Arc2Face Identity Unlearning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30087)
*Zeynel Tok*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文研究了基于人脸条件生成器的身份遗忘问题，发现通过重定向条件嵌入来遗忘身份可能会静默失败，失败的关键取决于重定向目标与遗忘身份在识别空间中的距离。在Arc2Face模型的严格测试中，目标选择策略呈现单调响应：使用最近邻目标时仅9/30组实现干净遗忘，而使用最不相似目标时可达到30/30，且平均遗忘身份重识别率从51.9降至0.0，同时保留性能保持稳定。研究还通过独立识别器AdaFace验证了该趋势并非识别器伪影，提出目标分离度是身份遗忘的首要可报告设计变量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于人脸条件生成器的身份遗忘方法通过重定向条件嵌入来实现，但可能静默失败——输出结果仍能被识别为原始人物。作者发现这种失败取决于重定向目标在识别空间中与遗忘身份的距离选择，且最直观的最近邻目标恰恰最容易导致失败，因此需要系统研究目标选择策略对遗忘效果的影响。

Method: 使用Arc2Face模型搭配锁定的ArcFace协议和投影适配器进行身份条件重定向。构建了从最难的0.5%合格身份组成的困难邻居压力测试集，比较四种目标选择策略（从最近邻到最不相似目标），并采用独立识别器AdaFace进行交叉验证，同时监控遗忘率、保留率和目标定向成功率等指标。

Result: 四种目标选择策略呈现单调响应：干净遗忘率从最近困难目标下的9/30组提升至最不相似目标下的30/30组；平均遗忘身份重识别率从51.9降至0.0；平均保留率保持稳定。在720个最不相似困难生成中，710个成功到达所选目标，无无关身份泄漏。AdaFace独立验证结果与ArcFace趋势高度相关（r=0.94），排除验证器伪影。

Conclusion: 目标分离度是身份遗忘中首要的、可报告的设计变量。研究者应当明确报告重定向目标与遗忘身份之间的距离选择，因为这一选择直接决定遗忘是否成功，而非仅依赖于生成器或验证器的选择。

Abstract: Unlearning an identity from a face-conditioned generator by redirecting its conditioning embedding can silently fail if the redirected output is still verified as the original person. We show that this failure depends on a controllable choice of how far the redirection target lies from the forget identity in recognition space, and that the most intuitive target, the nearest neighbour, is the one most likely to cause it. We audit Arc2Face with a locked ArcFace protocol and a projection adapter that redirects identity conditioning before generation. On a hard-neighbour stress test built from the hardest 0.5% of eligible identities, four target-selection policies show a monotonic response: clean forgetting rises from 9/30 groups under the nearest hard target to 30/30 under the least similar one. Mean forget-identity re-identification falls from 51.9 to 0.0 while mean retention stays flat. This reflects successful redirection rather than outputs becoming unverifiable: 710 of 720 least-sim-hard generations arrive at the chosen target, with no leakage to unrelated identities. Re-verifying identical images with an independent recogniser (AdaFace) preserves that trend, correlating at r=0.94, arguing against a verifier artefact. Target separation is thus a first-order, reportable design variable for identity unlearning.

</details>


### [167] [A Hybrid State-Space Approach for Census-Tract Population Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30094)
*Jackson R. Ye,Alexandre V. Morozov*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出MambaPop，将每个行政单元（普查区）表示为单个多边形掩膜的卫星图像，采用基于MambaVision混合状态空间-注意力架构的序列模型直接预测人口，绕过了传统的人口栅格化分解步骤。在美国约84,000个人口普查区的实验中，MambaPop达到每区MAE为1,141人，与最强卷积基线YOLOv11（MAE 1,122）相当。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的人口估算方法（如WorldPop、LandScan）依赖于将普查计数权重分配到规则栅格单元上，这一分解过程会引入系统性空间偏差，而且丢弃了普查数据实际收集时的行政单元结构。序列模型在人口估算任务中几乎没有被利用，作者希望弥合这一差距。

Method: MambaPop将每个行政单元渲染为单个多边形掩膜的卫星图像，将片区级人口估算视为图像块上的序列建模问题，直接将片区图像与人口标签配对，完全去除分解步骤。模型基于混合状态空间-注意力架构MambaVision构建。

Result: 在2020年美国人口普查约84,000个连续美国普查区上，MambaPop实现了每区MAE为1,141人，与最强卷积基线YOLOv11（MAE 1,122）表现相当。

Conclusion: MambaPop是首个直接从行政单元自身图像学习人口的方法，也是首个将Mamba混合架构应用于人口估算任务的工作，在性能上可与现有最佳卷积方法匹敌，同时消除了传统分解步骤带来的偏差来源。

Abstract: Sequence models---the architecture family behind large language models and, increasingly, state-of-the-art image recognition---have redefined how machines learn from high-dimensional data. Yet population estimation from satellite imagery, a task that underpins infrastructure planning, public health, and disaster response, has scarcely benefited: leading systems still bind population to a uniform raster, disaggregating census counts onto grid cells through weighting surfaces built from ancillary data (e.g., in WorldPop and LandScan), which can introduce systematic spatial bias, and predicting population per grid cell with convolutional neural networks. In this approach, the administrative-unit structure in which the census was actually collected is discarded. We close this gap with MambaPop, which renders each administrative unit as a single polygon-masked satellite image and treats tract-level population estimation as a sequence-modeling problem over its image patches, pairing each tract image directly with its population label and eliminating the disaggregation step entirely. Built on the hybrid state-space--attention MambaVision backbone, MambaPop is, to our knowledge, the first method to learn population directly from an administrative unit's own image as well as the first to apply a state-space based (Mamba) hybrid architecture to the population estimation task. Across all $\sim$84{,}000 contiguous-US census tracts of the 2020 census, MambaPop attains a mean absolute error (MAE) of $1{,}141$ persons per tract, matching the strongest convolutional baseline (YOLOv11, MAE $1{,}122$).

</details>


### [168] [Aligning Multi-Trajectory Supervision with Policy Optimization for VLA Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30122)
*Tian Zhang,Zhuo Huang,Hongrui Ye,Yu Wu,Zengmao Wang,Kaixuan Zhou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一个框架，将多轨迹监督与策略优化对齐，通过可行区域约束、Pareto最优性筛选、可行性优先优势分配和动态蒸馏，解决VLA驾驶中GRPO因不可行轨迹导致的策略梯度偏差问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的VLA驾驶方法将多轨迹模仿学习与GRPO结合时，部分高评分但不可行的轨迹会导致优势估计与当前策略的可行行为分布不匹配，使策略更新偏离安全和合规行为。

Method: 1) 将增广轨迹约束在地面真值可行区域附近的邻域流形上，并用Pareto最优性替代传统聚合评分，保留非支配候选以过滤冲突样本；2) 引入可行性优先优势分配，根据每个rollout组的可行性组成调整Pareto信用，引导完全不可行的组转向安全参考；3) 引入动态蒸馏机制，跨细化轮次更新教师轨迹以持续传递有效监督。

Result: 在NAVSIM v1和v2上，单轨迹推理下分别达到91.4 PDMS和89.1 EPDMS，恢复了658个初始失败场景中的440个，比原始GRPO基线高出11.1%。

Conclusion: 通过将扩展轨迹监督逐步转化为策略改进，该方法有效提升了VLA驾驶的性能与安全性，验证了多轨迹监督与策略优化对齐的重要性。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) driving methods increasingly combine multi-trajectory imitation learning with group-relative policy optimization (GRPO), making trajectory selection critical to final performance. However, some high-scoring trajectories that improve imitation can degrade subsequent GRPO by inducing advantage estimates misaligned with the current policy's feasible behavior distribution, driving updates away from safe and compliant behaviors. To address this, we propose a novel framework that aligns multi-trajectory supervision with policy optimization. To address the policy gradient bias induced by infeasible noisy trajectories outside the feasible region, augmented trajectories are constrained to a neighboring manifold of the ground-truth feasible region, and a Pareto-optimality criterion is adopted in place of the conventional aggregate score, retaining only non-dominated candidates and thereby filtering out conflicting samples at the source. To ensure that expanded trajectory supervision is effectively absorbed during policy optimization, we introduce two complementary mechanisms: feasibility-first advantage assignment and dynamic distillation. The former adapts Pareto credit to the feasibility composition of each rollout group and guides fully infeasible groups toward safe references. The latter updates teacher trajectories across refinement rounds to continually transfer useful supervision. Together, they progressively translate the benefits of expanded supervision into policy improvement. On NAVSIM v1 and v2, our method achieves 91.4 PDMS and 89.1 EPDMS, respectively, under single-trajectory inference, and recovers 440 of 658 initially failed scenes, 11.1\% higher than the original GRPO baseline.

</details>


### [169] [Efficient and High-Quality Depth Estimation via Pixel-Space Diffusion with Linear Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30129)
*Bingde Liu,Wu Ran,Jinglei Zhang,Huanhuan Yuan,Chao Ma*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Lapis，一种基于线性注意力的像素空间生成框架，通过一步扩散实现高效高保真的单目深度估计。采用从粗到细的层次结构：Patch级一致性模块恢复结构相干性，Pixel级细化模块恢复锐利几何边界，并利用流形假设采用直接x预测策略抑制一步扩散的采样噪声。在多个基准上达到SOTA精度和边界锐度，推理延迟在1080P降低7.6倍、1440P降低10.9倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式框架在单目深度估计中能实现优越的细节保真度，但标准注意力的O(N²)复杂度和多步去噪过程在高分辨率应用中带来高昂计算成本。虽然线性注意力和一步预测看似可行，直接使用会导致结构一致性差、细节丢失和噪声问题。

Method: Lapis采用粗到细的层次结构：(1) Patch级一致性模块通过整合语义和空间先验恢复结构相干性；(2) Pixel级细化模块通过基于跳跃连接的像素对应恢复锐利几何边界；(3) 利用流形假设采用直接x预测策略以目标干净数据流形，缓解一步扩散固有的采样噪声。

Result: 在多个基准上，Lapis在不同分辨率下一致达到SOTA精度和边界锐度；推理延迟在1080P分辨率下降低高达7.6倍，在1440P分辨率下降低10.9倍。

Conclusion: Lapis通过线性注意力和一步扩散的结合，在保证高质量深度估计的同时显著提升推理效率，验证了在高分辨率应用中生成式深度估计的实用可行性。

Abstract: This work presents $\textbf{Lapis}$, a $\textbf{l}$inear-$\textbf{a}$ttention-based $\textbf{pi}$xel-$\textbf{s}$pace generative framework that achieves efficient and high-fidelity depth estimation with one-step diffusion. While generative frameworks have significantly advanced monocular depth estimation with superior detail fidelity, the $\mathcal{O}(N^2)$ complexity of standard attention and the multi-step denoising process introduce prohibitive computational costs when scaling them to high-resolution image applications. Although linear attention and one-step prediction are intuitively viable, directly applying them leads to poor structural consistency, detail loss, and noise. Lapis rectifies these limitations through a coarse-to-fine hierarchy. Specifically, a Patch-level Consistency Module restores structural coherence by integrating semantic and spatial priors. Subsequently, a Pixel-level Refinement Module recovers sharp geometric boundaries via skip-connection-based pixel correspondence. Furthermore, to mitigate sampling noise inherent in one-step diffusion, we leverage the manifold assumption and adopt a direct $\mathbf{x}$-prediction strategy to target the clean data manifold. Extensive evaluations on multiple benchmarks demonstrate that Lapis consistently achieves state-of-the-art (SOTA) accuracy and boundary sharpness across various resolutions, reducing inference latency by up to 7.6$\times$ at 1080P and 10.9$\times$ at 1440P resolution compared to previous SOTA generative models.

</details>


### [170] [Repeatability Characterisation and Error Budget of a Consumer Structured-Light Scanner for 3-D Wound Geometry:A Rigid-Phantom Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30143)
*Pushkal Kumar,Aadit Aggarwal,Karlen Aleksanyan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文量化了自由手持式结构光扫描仪在三维伤口几何描述符测量中的误差。结果显示，重建表面积的重现性极限为4.8倍（95%置信区间3.0~7.0），45个描述符中有44个的组内相关系数低于0.50，无任何描述符/流程组合达到0.75；误差预算表明主要方差来源于操作者捕获表面范围，而非传感器本身。临床四周围积缩减比（约2.1）作为噪声下限，标准化分析区域后极限降至2.13但仍未达标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 伤口愈合评估依赖三维几何描述符，但自由手持式结构光扫描仪在伤口测量中的可重复性尚不明确，需要系统刻画测量误差及其来源，以判断此类设备的临床适用性与改进方向。

Method: 利用刚性伤口护理模具保证重复扫描的差异均属测量误差；在单一扫描仪上对9个部位进行23次重复扫描，计算95%重现性极限、组内相关系数（ICC），并通过固定分析区域构建误差预算，分解操作者捕获范围与传感器/重建环节的方差贡献。

Result: 重建表面积重现性极限为4.8倍（CI:3.0~7.0），表明未变化部位重扫读数可从-79%到+377%波动；45个描述符中44个ICC<0.50，248个组合无一达到0.75；操作者捕获范围在表面积、凸包面积、包围盒对角线方差中占75%/91%/95%，仅凸包体积以48%归因于仪器；标准化分析区域后重现性极限降至2.13，但统计异常值剔除造成约11%的系统性面积低估。

Conclusion: 该手持式结构光扫描仪的伤口测量可重复性极差，主要噪声源于操作者捕获表面范围而非传感器，仅靠升级硬件难有实质改善；当前未进行伤口分割的流程无法达到临床四周围积缩减比的可重复性要求，且异常值剔除引入系统性偏差，需在标准化操作与分析协议上做根本性改进。

Abstract: We characterise the measurement error of a free- hand consumer structured-light scanner used to derive three- dimensional geometric wound descriptors. Rigid wound-care phantoms cannot change, so every difference between repeat scans of one site is measurement error; all repeats come from a single scanner unit, nine sites and 23 scans, so this charac- terises one instrument. The 95 percent repeatability limit for reconstructed surface area is a factor of 4.8, with a confidence interval from 3.0 to 7.0, so rescanning an unchanged site can shift the reading from a 79 percent decrease to a 377 percent increase. Forty-four of 45 descriptors fall below an intraclass correlation of 0.50, and none of 248 descriptor and pipeline combinations reaches 0.75. An error budget formed by holding the analy- sis region fixed, leaving sensor and reconstruction untouched, bounds the share of variance attributable to how much surface the operator captured at 75 percent for surface area, 91 percent for hull area and 95 percent for bounding-box diagonal; only hull volume is majority instrumental, at 48 percent, so a better sensor would buy little. No wound is delineated anywhere in the chain, so the comparison against the four-week area reduction used clinically to predict healing, a ratio near 2.1, is a lower bound on the noise an unsegmented pipeline must overcome, not a measurement of wound-area reproducibility. Standardising the analysis region cuts the limit to 2.13, meeting that ratio rather than clearing it. Statistical outlier removal imposes a measured systematic area deficit near 11 percent.

</details>


### [171] [CedarCypress3D: an annotated UAV-LiDAR dataset of individual trees in planted cedar and cypress forests](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30149)
*Katsuto Shimizu,Fumiaki Kitahara,Tomohiro Nishizono,Hideki Saito,Masayoshi Takahashi,Shingo Obata,Shunsuke Tei,Naoyuki Furuya,Tomoya Goto,Eiji Kodani,Yusuke Yamada*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文介绍了CedarCypress3D，一个在日本柳杉和日本扁柏人工林中采集的、经过人工标注的UAV-LiDAR点云数据集。数据集包含日本两个不同地形特征场地的34个圆形样地，共1,627棵树的野外调查测量数据及对应的点云标注，其中22个样地还提供了地面LiDAR数据和语义标签（树干与非树干），可用于单木分割和语义分割方法的研究与评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 温带森林中公开可用的UAV-LiDAR标注数据集非常有限，而机器学习方法的发展需要大量高质量标注数据来开发和评估点云单木分割方法，因此亟需提供此类公开数据集以填补这一空白。

Method: 在日本两个不同地形特征的场地的34个圆形样地中采集UAV-LiDAR点云，通过野外调查测量1,627棵树并人工标注对应点云中的树木；对22个样地的子集，结合地面LiDAR数据额外分配语义标签（树干和非树干）。

Result: 成功构建了CedarCypress3D数据集，包含34个样地的UAV-LiDAR点云、1,627棵已标注树木的野外实测数据，以及22个样地的地面LiDAR数据和语义标注，并在Zenodo平台公开提供。

Conclusion: CedarCypress3D为温带人工林中单木实例分割和语义分割方法的开发与评估提供了高质量的标注数据集，同时也可支持树属性预测和多平台LiDAR分析等研究。

Abstract: Individual tree measurements derived from Light Detection and Ranging (LiDAR) mounted on Unmanned Aerial Vehicles (UAV) provide valuable information for forest inventory, ecosystem monitoring, and sustainable forest management. Recent advancements in machine learning have increased the demand for annotated datasets to develop and evaluate point cloud-based approaches, especially for individual tree segmentation. However, publicly available annotated UAV-LiDAR datasets in temperate forests are limited. In this article, we present CedarCypress3D, a manually annotated UAV-LiDAR dataset collected in Japanese cedar (Cryptomeria japonica) and Japanese cypress (Chamaecyparis obtusa) plantations in Japan. The dataset consists of UAV-LiDAR point clouds and field survey measurements from 34 circular plots across two sites with different topographic characteristics, along with terrestrial LiDAR point clouds available for a subset of 22 plots. A total of 1,627 trees were measured in the census field survey and manually annotated to match the corresponding trees in the UAV-LiDAR point clouds. For the subset of plots with terrestrial LiDAR data, semantic labels (i.e., stem and non-stem) were additionally assigned to tree points in the UAV-LiDAR data. CedarCypress3D provides high-quality annotated UAV-LiDAR data for developing and evaluating individual tree instance segmentation and semantic segmentation methods in temperate planted forests. The dataset can also support research on tree attribute prediction and multi-platform LiDAR analysis. The dataset is publicly available at https://doi.org/10.5281/zenodo.22168721.

</details>


### [172] [AI-enabled Low-Cost 3D Maize Ear Morphometry Platform at Breeding Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30161)
*Therin Young,Elijah Rodriguez,Lisa Coffey,Talukder Zaki Jubery,Adarsh Krishnamurthy,Patrick Schnable,Baskar Ganapathysubramanian*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文开发了一种低成本玉米穗3D表型采集管道，通过单段20秒的DSLR视频结合NeRF重建水密3D网格，在250个玉米穗上实现了与人工测量高度一致的骨架长度和凸包体积估算，硬件成本约607美元，操作时间从5分钟降至1分钟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 玉米穗几何特征（长度、宽度、曲率、体积）与产量和灌浆密切相关，但现有高通量表型分析管道受限于成本高、劳动密集和需要专用硬件，缺乏低成本的规模化解决方案。

Method: 使用消费级DSLR在电动转盘和均匀LED照明下拍摄20秒视频；通过多种子COLMAP初始化相机位姿，训练NeRF重建水密3D网格；利用每帧可见的已知直径圆柱支架实现自动度量缩放和质量控制；管道自动化处理下游流程。

Result: 300个玉米穗中250个（83.3%）通过自动处理和质量控制；骨架长度与卡尺测量R²=0.964，RMSE=4.68mm；凸包体积与水置换体积R²=0.982，RMSE=5.26mL；长度残差随曲率增加，但边界框高度无此趋势，差异源于测量定义（卡尺测弦长，骨架测测地线弧长）；硬件成本约607美元，操作时间从5分钟降至1分钟。

Conclusion: 该低成本自动化管道为育种规模的3D玉米穗表型分析奠定了基础，兼顾了硬件成本、操作效率和测量精度。

Abstract: Maize ear geometry (length, width, curvature, and volume) is closely tied to yield and grain-filling outcomes, but existing high-throughput phenotyping pipelines remain constrained by the cost, labor, and specialized hardware they require. We developed and validated a low-cost pipeline that reconstructs a watertight 3-D mesh of a maize ear from a single 20-second video captured with a consumer-grade DSLR on a motorized turntable under uniform LED illumination. Camera poses from a multi-seed COLMAP procedure initialize a Neural Radiance Field (NeRF), and a cylindrical holder of known diameter, visible in every frame, provides automatic metric scaling with downstream geometric quality control. Applied to 300 ears spanning a diverse maize inbred panel, 250 (83.3%) passed automated processing and quality control. Skeleton length agreed with manual caliper measurements across all 250 ears (R^2 = 0.964, RMSE = 4.68 mm), and convex-hull volume agreed with water-displacement volume on a 15-ear subset spanning the full size range (R^2 = 0.982, RMSE = 5.26 mL). Residual length error grew with ear curvature, whereas bounding-box height, which records the same straight-line chord as calipers, showed no such trend; the discrepancy therefore originates in the measurement definition, since calipers record the chord while skeleton length traces the geodesic arc. The capture hardware costs approximately 607 USD, and operator involvement fell from roughly five minutes to one minute per ear, with all downstream processing running unattended. The platform provides a foundation for breeding-scale 3-D ear phenotyping.

</details>


### [173] [ATGS: Anchored Temporal Gaussian Splatting for Long Volumetric Video Representation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30184)
*Jiahao Wu,Jie Liang,Die Hu,Jiayu Yang,Kaiqiang Xiong,Xiang Li,Xiaoyun Zheng,Chao Wang,Ronggang Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出ATGS，一种基于高斯泼溅的体视频重建框架，通过时间条件锚点组织和时间窗口策略来应对长序列和复杂运动导致的时序不稳定与视觉伪影问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有体视频方法在长序列和复杂运动场景下存在时序不稳定和视觉伪影问题，且直接用单个高斯基元显式跟踪长期复杂运动本质上是不稳定的。

Method: 1) 将高斯基元组织在时间条件锚点周围，通过锚点限定高斯基元的空间和时间支撑范围，降低长程运动复杂度；2) 提出时间窗口策略，仅激活与查询时间相关的锚点，提升可扩展性和时序一致性；3) 设计紧凑的多级锚点特征（全局特征、局部空间特征、局部时间特征）联合约束高斯生成，确保时空稳定性。

Result: 大量实验表明，ATGS在长序列和复杂运动的体视频重建上一致优于现有方法。

Conclusion: 通过时间条件锚点和时间窗口策略，ATGS有效解决了长序列复杂运动下的体视频重建稳定性和可扩展性问题，为体视频重建提供了一种新的高效框架。

Abstract: Volumetric video enables immersive free viewpoint rendering of dynamic real world scenes, yet existing methods struggle with long sequences and complex motions, often leading to temporal instability and visual artifacts. To address these challenges, we propose \ourname, a Gaussian splatting based framework for volumetric video reconstruction. Our key insight is that explicitly tracking long term complex motion with individual Gaussian primitives is inherently unstable. Instead, we organize Gaussians around time conditioned anchors that localize their spatial and temporal support, thereby reducing long range motion complexity. We further introduce a temporal windowing strategy to activate only anchors relevant to the queried time, which improves scalability and temporal coherence. In addition, to ensure spatial and temporal stability, we design a compact set of multi level anchor features that encode global features, local spatial features, and local temporal features, jointly constraining Gaussian generation. Extensive experiments demonstrate that \ourname \ consistently outperforms prior methods on long sequence volumetric videos with complex motions. Project page: https://github.com/WuJH2001/ATGS.

</details>


### [174] [NoisEasier: Test-Time Noise Optimization for Text-to-Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30194)
*Yujiang Pu,Yu Kong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出NoisEasier，一个测试时扩展框架，通过可微奖励引导的噪声优化提升文本到视频生成中的细粒度组合对齐能力，无需修改底层模型，在多个骨干网络上实现超过10%的平均性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的扩散模型在文本到视频生成中仍难以实现细粒度的组合对齐（如属性绑定、空间关系、物体交互）。奖励微调虽能改善对齐，但容易遭受奖励黑客攻击且难以适应新的提示分布。

Method: NoisEasier通过可微奖励引导的噪声优化实现测试时扩展，无需修改底层模型。关键创新在于将高效短步生成器与多目标奖励公式结合，并联合优化整个随机轨迹而非仅优化初始潜变量，以加速奖励收敛并改善组合对齐。

Result: 在VBench和T2V-CompBench基准上，NoisEasier在多个骨干网络上均展示出一致的改进，在属性绑定、物体交互和数值能力等具有挑战性的维度上平均提升超过10%，且额外计算和时间成本可忽略不计。

Conclusion: NoisEasier既可作为奖励微调的灵活替代方案，也可作为其互补增强，确立了测试时扩展作为可控文本到视频生成的有效范式。

Abstract: Diffusion models have recently advanced text-to-video (T2V) generation, yet they still struggle with fine-grained compositional alignment, such as attribute binding, spatial relations, and object interactions. While reward-based fine-tuning improves alignment, it is susceptible to reward hacking and adapts poorly to new prompt distributions. In this work, we propose NoisEasier, a test-time scaling framework that improves T2V generation through differentiable reward-guided noise optimization without modifying the underlying model. By combining efficient short-step generators with a multi-objective reward formulation, NoisEasier enables stable and practical test-time optimization under realistic inference budgets. Our key insight is that jointly optimizing the entire stochastic trajectory accelerates reward convergence and improves compositional alignment over optimizing only the initial latent, with negligible additional computational and time cost. Experiments on VBench and T2V-CompBench demonstrate consistent improvements across multiple backbones, achieving over 10% average gains on challenging dimensions such as attribute binding, object interaction, and numeracy.
