Conclusion: 该研究提供了一条有原则且经过验证的途径，促进了可靠神经运动接口的实际部署，证明了以数据为中心的策略能有效缓解边缘设备的资源约束。

Abstract: Dexterous human-machine interaction requires intuitive and expressive interfaces that can be efficiently deployed on constrained edge devices. Flexible material-based neuromotor interfaces hold considerable promise, as they decode human movement intention into natural control. Although emerging flexible electronic skins enable wearable high-fidelity data acquisition, practical deployment inevitably involves trade-offs between computational resources and portability. We present a data-centric paradigm where physiological features yield fundamental separability, providing sufficient discriminative cues for recognition. A wireless, high-bandwidth system developed for collecting various electrophysiological signals, when integrated with muscle-specific electrodes, forms a surface electromyography-based interface. Exploiting highly separable data, a 2,210-parameter model achieves 94.36% accuracy across 34 gestures and can be rapidly deployed on edge devices, establishing a new thousand-parameter benchmark for dexterous decoding. The underlying data-algorithm interactions in the data-centric paradigm are further clarified, demonstrating its feasibility in real-world scenarios. This study provides a principled and validated pathway for practical deployment of reliable neuromotor interfaces.

</details>


### [313] [SpectraTac: A Compact Camera-Free Optical Tactile Sensor with Distributed Color Sensing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30368)
*Hao Wu,Haotian Guo,Yu Feng,Yutong Wang,Yanzhe Wang,Jianshu Zhou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出SpectraTac，一种紧凑、无摄像头的光学触觉传感器，通过主动RGB照明与分布式颜色传感相结合，以低于5美元的成本实现高精度的3D力估计和接触区域分类，为机器人触觉感知提供了一种低计算开销的替代方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有触觉传感方法中，基于摄像头的方案虽然信息丰富但体积大、成本高、计算开销大，难以兼顾紧凑硬件与丰富触觉信息，需要一种低成本、紧凑且高效的触觉传感方案。

Method: SpectraTac采用主动RGB照明结合分布式颜色传感器，接触形变透明弹性体调制内部光场，产生颜色和强度的空间分化变化；三个分布式颜色传感器捕获低维时空光谱特征，无需摄像头和成像光学；通过数据驱动的解码框架同时估计3D力和接触区域。

Result: 3D力预测的MAE在x、y、z轴上分别为0.161、0.164和0.429 N；九区域接触分类准确率达99.9%；设备直径19.2mm、高4mm，材料成本低于5美元；在实时3D力追踪和基于接触区域的人机交互控制任务中得到验证。

Conclusion: 分布式颜色分辨光学传感为基于摄像头的触觉传感提供了一种紧凑、低成本的替代方案，适用于机器人、可穿戴传感和交互系统。

Abstract: Tactile sensing is essential for physical interaction in robotics and human--machine systems. However, combining rich tactile information with compact hardware, low cost, and low computational overhead remains challenging. This work presents SpectraTac, a compact, camera-free optical tactile sensor that combines active red--green--blue (RGB) illumination with spatially distributed color sensing. Contact deforms a compliant transparent elastomer and modulates its internal light field, producing spatially differentiated changes in color and intensity. Three distributed color sensors capture these responses as low-dimensional spatio-spectral features, avoiding cameras, imaging optics, and high-dimensional image processing. The device measures 19.2 mm in diameter and 4 mm in height, with a material cost below USD~5. A data-driven decoding framework simultaneously estimates three-dimensional (3D) force and the contact region from the optical measurements. For 3D force prediction, the sensor achieved mean absolute errors (MAEs) of 0.161, 0.164, and 0.429 N along the x-, y-, and z-axes, respectively. The nine-region contact-classification accuracy was 99.9%. We further evaluated real-time 3D force tracking and contact-region-based human--machine interaction through an interactive control task. These results indicate that distributed color-resolved optical sensing offers a compact, low-cost alternative to camera-based tactile sensing for robotics, wearable sensing, and interactive systems.

</details>


### [314] [PAVE: Predictive Alignment and Value-Guided Evolution for World-Action Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30378)
*Botong Zhao,Fang Yu,Tim,Senhua Zhu,Xinyuan Chen,Yue Lu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种直接世界-动作策略（\method），结合结果无关的预测学习与结果感知的策略改进，在保留直接动作生成路径的同时，在多时间尺度上学习场景演化并筛选优质轨迹，在三个仿真基准上取得最佳综合性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 直接视觉-语言-动作策略能高效生成连续机器人动作，但标准行为克隆存在两个互补的缺陷：一是其表示未被显式要求描述场景在多时间尺度上的演化；二是部署轨迹质量参差不齐，直接复用未区分有用动力学与不良行为。

Method: \method首先保留局部固定偏移的JEPA目标，并加入轨迹相对的多视野过渡对齐（在剩余回合的25%、50%、75%和100%处），这些训练目标要求当前策略表示同时保留局部物理变化和长程任务进度。随后训练一个独立的分布式价值评论员，基于累积部署轨迹计算动作块对齐的N步优势，并将其转化为正、负或空文本条件供流匹配actor使用。

Result: 在三个仿真基准上，\method取得了最强的综合性能，同时保留了直接actor的在线执行路径，多视野预测器和评论员在在线执行时被移除。

Conclusion: 通过分离"学习物理规律"与"学习优劣策略"两个任务，每个有效轨迹都能教给模型物理发生了什么，而actor仅在相对更优动作对应的条件下部署，从而在不增加在线推理开销的前提下提升策略质量。

Abstract: Direct vision-language-action policies generate continuous robot actions efficiently, but standard behavior cloning leaves two complementary gaps: their representations are not explicitly required to describe how the scene evolves over multiple time scales, and deployment trajectories of unequal quality are often reused without separating useful dynamics from undesirable behavior. We introduce \method, a direct world-action policy that combines outcome-agnostic predictive learning with outcome-aware policy improvement. \method first retains a local fixed-offset JEPA objective and adds trajectory-relative multi-horizon transition alignment at 25%, 50%, 75%, and 100% of the remaining episode. These training-only targets require the current policy representation to preserve both local physical changes and longer-range task progress, without supplying explicit future tokens to the action head. \method then trains an independent distributional value critic on cumulative deployment trajectories, computes action-chunk-aligned $N$-step advantages, and converts them into positive, negative, or null text conditions for a flow-matching actor. Thus, every valid trajectory can teach what physically happened, while the actor is deployed only under the condition associated with relatively better actions. The multi-horizon predictor and critic are removed from online execution, preserving direct action generation from the current observation, language instruction, and proprioception. \redclaim{Across the three simulation benchmarks, \method achieves the strongest overall performance while preserving the direct actor's online execution path.}

</details>


### [315] [A Hybrid PEM-GP Framework for Uncertainty-Aware System Identification of Quadcopters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30433)
*Abdallah Ghoul,Ismail Khalil Bousserhane,Kadri Boufeldja*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种融合物理建模与数据驱动学习的四旋翼无人机动力学建模框架，先通过预测误差方法（PEM）识别物理模型，再用高斯过程（GP）学习残差动态，在Duckiedrone实验平台上验证，结果表明该方法在预测精度上与LSTM相当，同时能提供校准的不确定性估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统系统辨识方法虽然可解释性强，但难以捕捉小型四旋翼平台中显著的非线性效应；纯数据驱动方法表达能力更强，但缺乏可解释性和校准的不确定性估计。因此需要一种兼顾两者优点的建模框架。

Method: 采用两阶段建模框架：首先使用预测误差方法（PEM）辨识基于物理的动力学模型以捕捉系统主要结构，然后将剩余动态用高斯过程（GP）从数据中学习，从而明确区分已知物理效应与未建模动态，并输出不确定性估计。

Result: 在Duckiedrone-like实验平台上验证，PEM-GP模型的预测精度与LSTM网络相当，同时具备校准的不确定性估计能力，提升了模型的可靠性。

Conclusion: PEM-GP混合建模框架有效结合了物理模型的解释性与数据驱动方法的表达能力，在保证预测精度的同时提供不确定性量化，可支持不确定性感知的决策，提升了四旋翼动力学建模的可靠性。

Abstract: Accurate dynamic models play a central role in achieving reliable control of quadcopters. Classical system identification methods remain widely used, mainly because of their interpretability. However, they often fail to capture important nonlinear effects, especially in small-scale aerial platforms where such effects become more pronounced.
  Data-driven approaches offer a different perspective. They can represent complex nonlinear dynamics more effectively, but this comes at the cost of reduced interpretability and the absence of well-calibrated uncertainty estimates.
  In this work, we propose a framework that combines physics-based modeling with data-driven learning, while explicitly accounting for uncertainty. A physics-based model is first identified using the Prediction Error Method (PEM), which captures the main structure of the system. The remaining dynamics are then modeled using a Gaussian Process (GP), allowing the residual behavior to be learned directly from data. This separation makes it possible to distinguish between known physical effects and unmodeled dynamics.
  The proposed framework is validated on a Duckiedrone-like experimental setup. The results show that the PEM-GP model achieves prediction accuracy comparable to that of a Long Short-Term Memory (LSTM) network, while additionally providing calibrated uncertainty estimates. This combination improves model reliability and supports uncertainty-aware decision-making.

</details>


### [316] [Anomaly Detection on Small Industrial Components via Vision-Based Tactile Sensing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30506)
*G. F. Preziosa,M. Casiglia,M. Faroni,A. M. Zanchettin,P. Rocco*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文系统评估了基于视觉的触觉传感器在工业组件异常检测中的应用，使用GelSight Mini在协作机器人上获取五个真实工业组件的触觉数据集，比较了SPADE、PaDiM、FAPM和InReaCh四种无监督异常检测方法，在三种贴合部署约束的验证条件下进行基准测试，为该模态的工业质检应用提供实用指导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 小型工业组件的自动化检测面临挑战，尤其是亚厘米级部件中由几何形状驱动的缺陷难以被标准光学相机有效捕捉。虽然基于视觉的触觉传感器能将接触印记转化为高分辨率图像数据并兼容现有深度学习管线，但其在工业异常检测中的应用尚未被系统探索。

Method: 在装配了GelSight Mini传感器的协作机器人上，对五个真实工业组件获取触觉数据集。比较四种特征嵌入方法（SPADE、PaDiM、FAPM、InReaCh），并在三种由接触式传感部署约束明确驱动的验证条件下评估：Good Fraction分析（确定稳定性能所需的最小接触次数，受凝胶磨损限制）、跨位置评估（测试不同接触位置下的泛化能力）、低分辨率与高分辨率对比（评估高分辨率触觉采集的成本效益）。

Result: 系统性地比较了四种无监督异常检测方法在真实触觉数据集上的性能，明确了最小名义接触次数对稳定性能的要求，评估了跨接触位置的泛化能力，并量化了高分辨率触觉采集的收益与成本权衡。

Conclusion: 该基准研究为采用视觉触觉传感进行工业异常检测的研究者和从业者提供了实用指导，表明该模态可作为工业质检任务中可行的替代方案。

Abstract: Automated inspection of small industrial components, including sub-centimetre-scale parts where defects are geometry-driven and poorly resolved by standard optical cameras, calls for sensing modalities that can directly capture fine surface geometry. Vision-based tactile sensors address this need by converting contact imprints into high-resolution image-like data compatible with existing deep-learning pipelines, yet their effective use for industrial anomaly detection (AD) remains largely unexplored. This work systematically evaluates unsupervised AD methods on a real tactile dataset covering five genuine industrial components acquired with a GelSight Mini sensor mounted on a collaborative robot. Four feature-embedding methods, SPADE, PaDiM, FAPM, and InReaCh, are compared under three validations explicitly motivated by the deployment constraints of contact-based sensing: a Good Fraction analysis establishing the minimum number of nominal contacts for stable performance, directly bounded by gel wear since every acquisition degrades the soft interface; a cross-position evaluation assessing generalization across different contact locations observing the same recurring surface pattern; and a low- versus high-resolution comparison evaluating the cost-benefit of higher-resolution tactile acquisition. Overall, this systematic benchmarking study provides practical guidance for researchers and practitioners adopting vision-based tactile sensing for industrial AD and shows how this modality can serve as a viable alternative for industrial quality-control tasks.

</details>


### [317] [Behavior-Skill: A Fine-Grained Benchmark for Evaluating Vision-Language-Action Policies in Long-Horizon Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30536)
*Chunyun Ma,Lun Luo,Xingjian Luo,Xiexing Feng,Hang Zhang,Wei Liu,Feng Qiao,Yaonan Wang,Huimin Lu,Xieyuanli Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出Behavior-Skill基准，将长时程移动操作任务的评估从整体任务层面重构为可执行的构成性技能层面，包含235,492个技能实例（来自50个家庭任务、10,000条演示、34个语义技能类别），并引入轨迹级和技能级指标以揭示技能层面的失败模式。实验发现接触丰富的操作技能是VLA策略的持续瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长时程移动操作任务的可靠执行具有挑战性，因为整体任务成功依赖于多个构成技能的依次完成。然而现有基准主要依赖完整任务回放和聚合任务级指标，导致中间失败难以观察和分析，缺乏对技能层面的细粒度评估能力。

Method: 论文构建Behavior-Skill基准，将长时程任务重构为可执行的构成技能：(1) 从10,000条演示中提取235,492个技能实例，覆盖50个家庭任务和34个语义技能类别；(2) 每个实例包含技能指令、对齐的观测-动作片段、可恢复的中间状态和技能成功条件，支持在有效前置条件下独立评估；(3) 引入轨迹级和技能级指标以刻画策略能力，超越聚合任务成功率。

Result: 在完整50任务基准上对pi0.5和GR00T等代表性VLA策略的广泛实验表明，失败在技能间高度不均匀分布，接触丰富的操作技能构成持续瓶颈。

Conclusion: Behavior-Skill作为全任务评估的补充，通过暴露中间能力画像，为分析和改进长时程VLA策略提供了有力工具。基准已公开于GitHub。

Abstract: Reliable execution of long-horizon mobile manipulation tasks remains challenging because overall task success depends on the successful completion of multiple constituent skills. Existing benchmarks, however, still rely primarily on full-task rollouts and aggregate task-level metrics, making intermediate failures difficult to observe and analyze. We present Behavior-Skill, a benchmark that reformulates the learning and evaluation of long-horizon tasks around executable constituent skills. It contains 235,492 skill instances from 10,000 demonstrations across 50 household tasks and 34 semantic skill categories. Each instance pairs a skill instruction with an aligned observation-action segment, and is further associated with a restorable intermediate state and a skill success condition to enable independent evaluation under valid preconditions. We further introduce trajectory-level and skill-level metrics to characterize policy capability beyond aggregate task success. Extensive experiments across representative VLA policies including pi0.5 and GR00T on the complete 50-task benchmark show that failures are highly non-uniform across skills, with contact-rich manipulation skills forming persistent bottlenecks. These results demonstrate that Behavior-Skill complements full-task evaluation by exposing intermediate capability profiles for analyzing and improving long-horizon VLA policies. Behavior-Skill is publicly available at https://github.com/nubot-nudt/Behavior-Skill.

</details>


### [318] [Temporal Forcing: 4D Representation Alignment for Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30643)
*Xingyu Ding,Yuzhong Zhao,Chunhai Zhao,Yinghuan Shi,Chaoyang Zhao,Yifan Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出Temporal Forcing方法，通过引入历史路径并将VLA模型的潜在表示与预训练4D基础模型的几何特征对齐，使模型能够理解随时间演化的三维世界，从而解决长时程操作任务中因缺乏时间信息导致的观测混淆问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的视觉-语言-动作（VLA）方法虽然通过将表示与3D场景几何对齐提升了操作性能，但在长时程操作任务中容易因缺乏时间信息而失效，且难以区分视觉上相似但语义不同的状态。3D场景几何只捕捉当前状态，无法反映状态随时间的变化过程。

Method: 1）引入历史路径（history pathway），使基础VLA模型能够将观测历史总结为时间感知的潜在表示；2）将这些潜在表示与预训练4D基础模型提取的几何特征进行对齐，4D模型通过时间一致的几何表示捕捉动态演化的3D世界。

Result: 在LIBERO基准上达到98.8%的成功率，比基础模型提升2.2个百分点；在物理隐藏摆放任务上，全任务成功率从20.0%提升至43.3%。

Conclusion: 通过4D表示对齐引入时间维度信息，能显著提升VLA模型在长时程操作和观测混淆场景下的表现，验证了时间感知表示对于动态环境理解的重要性。

Abstract: Recent vision-language-action (VLA) methods improve manipulation performance by aligning their representations with 3D scene geometry. However, these methods often struggle with long-horizon manipulation and observation aliasing between visually similar states due to a lack of temporal information: the 3D scene geometry captures only the current state, rather than how it has evolved over time. To resolve this, we present Temporal Forcing, a 4D representation alignment method for VLA models. Specifically, we first introduce a history pathway that enables a vanilla VLA model to summarize observation history into temporally aware latent representations. Then, the latent representations are aligned with the geometric features extracted by a pretrained 4D foundation model, which captures the evolving 3D world through temporally consistent geometric representations, enabling a deeper understanding of dynamic environments. Temporal Forcing reaches 98.8% on LIBERO, outperforming its base model by 2.2 points. On a physical hidden-placement task, it raises full-task success from 20.0% to 43.3%. Code will be publicly available.

</details>


### [319] [CIG-RL: Curiosity-Driven Information-Guided Reinforcement Learning for Source Term Estimation in Uncertain Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30673)
*Junhee Lee,Seunghwan Kim,Hongro Jang,Hyungjin Kim,Hyoungho Park,Changseung Kim,Hyondong Oh*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种好奇心驱动的信息引导强化学习方法（curiosity-driven information-guided reinforcement learning）用于气体源项估计（STE），通过促进对未充分探索的新颖信念状态转移的主动探索，并结合不确定性自适应的主动感知奖励，在噪声环境下实现了稳健高效的气体源搜索。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于深度强化学习（DRL）的源项估计方法存在两大局限：一是依赖随机探索，策略在噪声环境下鲁棒性不足；二是仅基于信念不确定度降低来引导动作选择，缺乏有效的探索策略，限制了模型在真实噪声环境中的表现。

Method: 提出好奇心驱动的信息引导强化学习框架，包含两个核心组件：(1) 好奇心驱动的探索机制，鼓励智能体主动探索训练过程中未充分覆盖的新颖信念状态转移；(2) 不确定性自适应的主动感知奖励，在不确定性环境下引导高效的气体源搜索。

Result: 在高噪声条件下的仿真实验以及真实世界实验中均验证了所提框架的鲁棒性和可行性，展示了其在实际源项估计问题中的应用潜力。

Conclusion: 好奇心驱动的信息引导强化学习方法有效解决了现有DRL-STE方法在噪声环境下的探索不足问题，兼顾了决策效率和估计鲁棒性，为实际危险气体泄漏源定位提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Source term estimation (STE), which aims to estimate key properties of the gas source, is essential for identifying hazardous gas releases. Information-theoretic approaches have been adopted for autonomous STE using mobile sensors due to robustness in noisy environments, yet their online action selection incurs substantial computational cost. Deep reinforcement learning (DRL) provides a promising alternative with its fast decision-making capability. In DRL-based STE, the agent selects actions based on belief states of the source term updated from noisy measurement sequences. However, existing methods rely on random exploration or solely on belief uncertainty reduction without an effective exploration strategy in DRL, which can limit policy robustness in noisy environments. To address this, we propose a curiosity-driven information-guided reinforcement learning for robust and efficient STE. The proposed method promotes active exploration of novel belief state transitions that have not been sufficiently explored during training. We further introduce an uncertainty-adaptive active perception reward to guide efficient source search under uncertainty. Simulations under high-noise conditions and real-world experiments demonstrate the robustness and feasibility of the proposed framework, highlighting its potential for practical STE problems.

</details>


### [320] [Learning to infer and manipulate through distributed whole-arm interaction in a soft robot](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30773)
*Chuhan Zhang,Ebrahim Shahabi,Kseniia Khomenko,Wei Pan,Cosimo Della Santina*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种"物理智能"框架，让机器人在与环境的分布式柔性交互中同时获取任务信息和组织操作行为，并通过基于记忆的强化学习策略，在仅有本体感觉（IMU）的条件下实现盲态整臂抓取。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前机器人智能对物理交互的利用极为有限，通常将交互视为需要拒绝的扰动，或至多用于补偿物体错位，未能像大象、章鱼等动物那样将感知与操作融为一体。

Method: 提出物理智能框架，将分布式柔性交互作为信息获取与行为组织的共同媒介，构建内在部分可观测的强化学习问题，采用基于记忆的端到端控制策略，包含三大创新：预训练探索策略提供工作空间广泛探索的参考、将探索与抓取目标在单一循环策略中联合优化、以及包含观测映射与策略微调的两阶段仿真到现实迁移。

Result: 在配备嵌入式IMU的混合刚柔机械臂上，学习策略能够自主协调工作空间探索、物体遭遇与定位、抓取相关属性推断以及稳定整臂包裹，成功识别并抓取多种物体。

Conclusion: 该工作证明了仅通过本体感觉（IMU）和物理交互，无需视觉等外部感知，就能实现复杂物体的识别与抓取，为软体机器人的物理智能提供了可行路径。

Abstract: In animals such as elephants and octopuses, acquiring non-visual information about an object and physically engaging with it are inseparable processes mediated by rich, large-area interactions between compliant appendages and the environment. Soft robots provide a natural platform for translating this principle into engineered systems. Yet current robotic intelligence makes limited use of physical interaction, treating it primarily as a disturbance to be rejected or, at best, as a means of compensating for object misalignment.
  Here, we introduce a physical intelligence framework in which distributed compliant interactions jointly reveal task-relevant information and organize manipulation behavior. This results in an intrinsically partially observable problem: key task-relevant information is never measured directly, but must instead be inferred from the history of physical interactions. We propose a reinforcement-learning architecture that addresses this challenge by learning a memory-based control policy end-to-end. The key innovations making this possible are (i) a pretrained exploration policy that provides a reference for broad workspace exploration, (ii) joint optimization that integrates exploration and grasping objectives within a single recurrent policy, and (iii) a two-stage sim-to-real adaptation including observation mapping and policy fine-tuning.
  We demonstrate this principle through blind whole-arm grasping with a hybrid rigid-soft robotic arm that we equip with IMUs embedded directly within its compliant structure, providing its only source of proprioceptive sensing. The learned policy successfully identifies and grasps various objects by autonomously coordinating workspace exploration, object encounter and localization, inference of grasp-relevant properties, and stable whole-arm wrapping.

</details>


### [321] [A Dual-Cam Parallel Elastic Actuator with Shared Gas-Spring Compensation for Humanoid Ankles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30832)
*Jingcheng Jiang,Yifang Zhang,Nikos G. Tsagarakis*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种用于人形机器人踝关节的2自由度并联弹性执行器（PEA），其核心创新在于双凸轮-单气弹簧架构，通过共享弹性元件实现俯仰和横滚两个方向的扭矩补偿，提升结构紧凑性。同时建立了耦合的2自由度数学模型，并基于优化框架设计定制化凸轮轮廓，实现从任务需求到硬件实现的系统性映射。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 提升人形机器人踝关节的扭矩容量和能量效率，解决传统多弹性元件补偿方案结构不紧凑、难以实现双轴扭矩补偿的问题。

Method: 提出双凸轮-单气弹簧架构的2自由度并联弹性执行器；建立耦合2自由度数学模型以刻画共享弹簧带来的两个补偿单元间的相互依赖；开发基于优化的设计框架，根据预设扭矩参考合成定制凸轮轮廓；完成下肢CAD集成设计，并进行静态有限元分析和运动学仿真验证。

Result: 静态有限元分析和运动学仿真确认了设计的可行性和扭矩缓解效果，验证了该方案作为紧凑、可定制的2自由度人形机器人踝关节扭矩补偿方案的有效性。

Conclusion: 所提出的双凸轮-单气弹簧PEA架构是一种结构紧凑、高度可定制的2自由度踝关节扭矩补偿解决方案，能够通过共享弹性元件实现双轴扭矩辅助，并通过优化框架实现任务需求到硬件设计的系统化映射。

Abstract: To improve torque capacity and energy efficiency of humanoid ankles, this paper proposes a 2-DoF parallel elastic actuator (PEA). The main novelty of the proposed design lies in its dual-cam, single-gas-spring architecture, which enables torque compensation in both pitch and roll using a shared elastic element, thereby improving structural compactness compared with conventional multi-element compensation schemes. By leveraging parallel gas springs and customized cam modules, the proposed architecture provides dual-axis torque assistance tailored to specific task requirements. The second key contribution is the formulation of a coupled 2-DoF mathematical model that explicitly captures the interdependence between the two compensation units through the shared spring. Based on this model, an optimization-based design framework is developed to synthesize customized cam profiles from prescribed torque references, establishing a systematic link from task requirements to hardware realization. The complete lower-leg CAD integration is presented in detail. Static FEA and kinematic simulations confirm the design's feasibility and torque-relief effectiveness. The results highlight the proposed design as a compact, customizable solution for 2-DoF humanoid ankle torque compensation.

</details>


### [322] [GAFT: Geo-Anchored Fine-Tuning for Hazard Identification from Rare Failures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30858)
*Yanran Xu,Chuanhang Qiu,Yue Wang,Wenbo Wu,Zhaoxing Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出Geo-Anchored Fine-Tuning (GAFT)方法，用几何先验引导视觉基础模型的LoRA微调，在越野导航危险结构识别任务上显著提升性能（F2从0.0607提升至0.3757），解决了失败数据稀少且缺乏视觉线索标注的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 越野导航中物理结构（如高底盘卡住或困陷）会导致不可恢复状态且需要人工干预，因此识别这些结构至关重要。但这类失败事件罕见且收集成本高，训练数据有限；且数据只关联帧与结果，没有标注导致失败的视觉线索，直接学习会利用场景特定的视觉线索导致泛化能力差。

Method: 提出Geo-Anchored Fine-Tuning (GAFT)，一种参数高效方法，利用几何推导的先验来适配视觉基础模型。通过将空间注意力rollout图与几何先验对齐来引导LoRA适配，同时保留预训练表示。

Result: 在干预验证的森林危险基准上，跨十个独立训练适配，GAFT一致优于冻结的DINOv2和有监督PEFT基线，将重复留一场景外均值F2从0.0607提升到0.3757，配对分析具有统计显著性；表现最好的GAFT模型达到重复LOSO F2为0.570。

Conclusion: GAFT通过几何先验引导参数高效的微调，有效解决了失败数据稀缺和缺乏视觉线索标注的问题，在越野导航危险结构识别任务上取得了显著且稳定的性能提升，证明了该方法在罕见失败事件识别中的有效性。

Abstract: Off-road navigation can fail when physical structures induce irrecoverable states such as high-centering or entrapment, requiring human interventions. Identifying these structures is crucial, yet challenging. Such failure events are rare and costly to collect, resulting in limited training data. Moreover, the collected data associate frames with outcomes, but do not indicate the visual cues responsible for the failure. Learning directly from these data can therefore exploit scenario-specific visual cues, leading to poor generalization. We propose \textbf{Geo-Anchored Fine-Tuning (GAFT)}, a parameter-efficient method that adapts a vision foundation model with a geometry-derived prior. It guides LoRA adaptation by aligning a spatial attention-rollout map with the geometry prior, while preserving pretrained representations. On an intervention-verified forest hazard benchmark, across ten independently trained adaptations, GAFT consistently outperforms frozen DINOv2 and supervised PEFT baselines, improving the repeated leave-one-scenario-out mean $F_2$ from 0.0607 to 0.3757 with statistical significance under paired analysis. Within these independently trained models, the best-performing GAFT model achieves a repeated-LOSO $F_2$ of 0.570. Code and benchmark: https://github.com/Xu-Yanran/geo_anchored_fine_tuning

</details>


### [323] [Zeva: In-Context Causal Learning for Generalizable Embodied Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30880)
*Fu Chen,Xin Ding,Bingjia Huang,Xiangyu Li,Mingju Wang,Jiawei He,Kun Li,Wei Sun,Yunxin Liu,Hao Wu,Ting Cao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Zeva是首个实现在部署过程中从机器人自身物理交互经验中进行上下文学习的框架，无需更新策略模型参数。它通过因果交互提取器编码动作和状态变化，存储在双时间尺度因果记忆中，并在后续动作中检索相关经验注入冻结的策略模型。实验表明Zeva在仿真和真实操控中优于前沿VLA和WAM方法，并能在无需梯度更新的情况下实现自我进化，成功率随经验积累持续提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 仅通过预训练难以实现通用可泛化的具身操控，因为真实世界中存在未见的物理条件。机器人需要在真实部署过程中从自身物理交互中在线学习，并利用这些知识指导后续动作。

Method: Zeva框架由两部分组成：1）因果交互提取器，将已执行的动作及其引发的状态变化编码为因果交互信号；2）双时间尺度因果记忆，用于存储和检索相关因果交互信号，并将其作为上下文注入冻结的策略模型中以指导后续动作。

Result: 在仿真和真实操控实验中，Zeva在对比的前沿VLA和WAM方法中取得最佳性能。机器人在部署过程中无需梯度更新即可实现自我进化，成功率随交互经验积累持续提升，且获得的交互经验可跨任务泛化。

Conclusion: 通过将机器人自身的物理交互经验以因果信号形式存储并作为上下文注入冻结策略模型，可以在无需参数更新的情况下实现部署过程中的持续学习和自我进化，为通用具身操控提供了一种新的范式。

Abstract: Generalizable embodied manipulation remains difficult to achieve through pretraining alone, due to unseen physical conditions in the real world. We argue that robots need to learn from their own physical interactions on the fly during real-world deployment and use this knowledge to inform subsequent actions. We present Zeva, the first framework that enables in-context learning from a robot's own physical interaction experience while keeping the policy model frozen. Zeva employs a Causal Interaction Extractor to encode an executed action and its induced state change into a causal interaction signal, which is stored in a dual-timescale causal memory. For subsequent actions, relevant causal interaction signals are retrieved from memory and injected into the frozen policy model as context. Experiments in simulation and real-world manipulation demonstrate that Zeva achieves the best performance among the compared frontier VLAs and WAMs and, more importantly, enables self-evolution during deployment without gradient updates. Its success rate continues to improve as the robot accumulates interaction experience. Furthermore, the acquired interaction experience can generalize across tasks.

</details>


### [324] [SleepWalking: Privileged Representation Shaping for End-to-End Blind Locomotion in Legged Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30883)
*Zheng Pan,Tenghui Wang,Peilin Li,Shiyu Zhou,Hao Sun,Yan Ma,Liang Yu,Liang He*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出SWAQ框架，通过下一时刻的特权物理重建来塑造循环历史表征的信息保留，在部分可观测的机器人行走任务中，相比最强基线DWAQ实现了15.0%更高的峰值地形难度，同时推理计算量减少44.4%，表明语义目标可以在无需部署控制器架构分解的情况下结构化学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 部分可观测的机器人行走需要策略在瞬时观测无法完全确定机器人-环境状态时做出决策。现有方法要么显式估计缺失的物理变量，要么通过结构化架构处理扩展的观测历史。作者提出不同观点：部分可观测性本质上是信息保留问题，关键在于策略内部状态是否能保留任务相关信息，而非信息如何进入网络。

Method: SWAQ是一个单阶段端到端框架，使用下一时刻特权物理重建来塑造循环历史表征在学习过程中的信息保留，部署时的actor仅使用直接的历史到动作通路。方法包括层探针分析验证物理变量信息的线性可解码性，以及将特权变量可恢复性与基于历史和特权信息策略类之间可实现回报差距相联系的理论分析。

Result: 在对齐的训练设置下，SWAQ相比最强非外感受基线DWAQ峰值平均地形水平提高15.0%，每控制步推理MACs减少44.4%。层探针显示与重建物理变量相关的信息在策略头部直到动作输出前的层都保持线性可解码。

Conclusion: 语义感知目标可以在不需要部署控制器进行相应架构分解的情况下结构化学习过程。部分可观测性作为信息保留问题来处理是有效的，通过合理的目标设计可以实现更高效且更强大的策略。

Abstract: Partially observable locomotion requires a policy to act when task-relevant properties of the robot--environment state are not fully specified by instantaneous observations. Existing approaches often address this challenge by explicitly estimating missing physical variables or processing extended observation histories through structured architectures. We take a different view: partial observability is fundamentally an information-retention problem. The decisive question is not how task-relevant information enters the network, but whether the policy's internal state retains it. Guided by this perspective, we propose SleepWalking for Robot Locomotion (SWAQ), a one-stage end-to-end framework that uses next-step privileged physical reconstruction to shape what a recurrent history representation retains during policy learning, while the deployed actor uses only a direct history-to-action pathway. Under aligned training settings, SWAQ achieves a 15.0\% higher peak mean terrain level than DWAQ, the strongest non-exteroceptive baseline, while using 44.4\% fewer inference MACs per control step. Layerwise probes further show that information associated with the reconstructed physical variables remains linearly decodable through the policy head up to the layer preceding the action output. Complementary theoretical analysis relates privileged-variable recoverability to the achievable-return gap between history-based and privileged-information policy classes. These results suggest that semantic objectives can structure learning without requiring a corresponding architectural decomposition of the deployed controller.

</details>


### [325] [LightNav-0: Eliciting VLM Spatial Intelligence for Generalist Embodied Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30935)
*Shaoan Wang,Aocheng Luo,Fei Huang,Jingyi Xu,Xiaoyang Wang,Yueyu Wang,Qianli Ma,Fan Yang,Ran Mei,Jia Wei,Jiangpeng Hu,Xuhao Liu,Hongming Chen,Yuanbin Shao,Yiyang Lin,Ziliang Li,Liang Pan,Xinhang Liu,Yuntao Ma,Tingxiang Fan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出LightNav-0，一个紧凑的通用具身导航模型，通过统一Token接口（双通道指向+残差向量量化动作分词器）直接激发预训练VLM的空间智能用于导航控制，无需任务特定预测头。在8个具身推理基准和10个导航仿真设置上取得SOTA，并实现跨机器人形态、场景和目标的零样本泛化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有具身导航系统依赖任务或形态特定的组件，将感知、推理和行动割裂，泛化能力有限。尽管现代VLM已编码丰富的空间先验（视觉定位、空间推理、指向），但这些能力很少被直接用于机器人控制，因此需要一种能统一激发VLM空间智能的通用导航模型。

Method: LightNav-0采用统一Token接口：双通道指向编码任务/场景/形态无关的空间意图，残差向量量化动作分词器将意图映射为精确的形态特定轨迹。结合时序感知的视觉历史压缩、ER中训练、监督微调和强化学习，支持指令跟随、开放词汇目标导航和视觉跟踪。训练语料涵盖2K+场景和4K+小时的具身导航数据。

Result: LightNav-ER在8个具身推理基准上取得最高完整集平均值；LightNav-0在全部10个公开导航仿真设置中达到SOTA单目成功率；真实世界评估展示了跨机器人形态、多样场景以及静态/动态目标的零样本泛化能力。

Conclusion: 结果表明紧凑型VLM可以成为通用具身导航的统一且可迁移的骨干网络，为建立轻量级、跨任务和跨形态的通用导航智能体提供了一条可行路径。

Abstract: Embodied navigation requires agents to translate heterogeneous goals and visual observations into actions across tasks, environments, and robot embodiments. Modern vision-language models (VLMs) already encode spatial priors for visual grounding, spatial reasoning, and pointing, but these capabilities are rarely elicited directly for robot control. Existing navigation systems instead rely on task- or embodiment-specific components, fragmenting perception, reasoning, and action while offering limited generalization. Here we present LightNav-0, a compact generalist embodied navigation model that elicits the spatial intelligence of a pretrained VLM and aligns it with navigation, without task-specific prediction heads. LightNav-0 represents diverse navigation tasks through a unified token interface: dual-channel pointing expresses task-, scene-, and embodiment-agnostic spatial intent, while a residual vector-quantized action tokenizer maps this intent to precise, embodiment-specific trajectories. Together with temporally aware visual history compression, ER mid-training, supervised fine-tuning, and reinforcement learning, this formulation supports instruction following, open-vocabulary object navigation, and visual tracking within a single model. The navigation training corpus spans 2K+ scenes and 4K+ hours of embodied navigation data. LightNav-ER, the embodied-reasoning checkpoint used to initialize LightNav-0, attains the highest complete-set average across 8 embodied-reasoning benchmarks, while LightNav-0 achieves state-of-the-art monocular success rates across all 10 public navigation simulation settings. Real-world evaluations further demonstrate zero-shot generalization across robot embodiments, diverse scenes, and static and dynamic targets. These results establish compact VLMs as a unified and transferable backbone for generalist embodied navigation.

</details>


### [326] [Autonomously Acquiring Robot Manipulation Skills with Language-Driven Quality-Diversity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30983)
*Émiland Garrabé,Mahdi Khoramshahi,Stéphane Doncieux*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种利用大语言模型（LLM）自动设计适应度与多样性指标的方法，使质量-多样性（QD）算法无需专家干预即可生成多样化的运动原语库，在4个机器人操作任务上优于经典的QD算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的QD算法在机器人学习中有优势，但需要专家手动编写成功条件、适应度和多样性指标，限制了机器人的自主性；而基于LLM的奖励塑形技术虽可实现自主学习，但只输出单一高表现解，缺乏适应性。作者希望结合两者优势，实现仅凭自然语言任务描述即可自主输出多样化运动原语库的目标。

Method: 提出自主探索机制，将策略探索建模为函数设计问题，利用低维函数空间（低于完整BD和适应度空间）进行采样；设计基于LLM的探索方案，无需任务特定提示、微调或专家干预；并改编了MAP-Elites成功（MES）算法的多BD变体，以利用异构的BD样本。

Result: 基于genesis模拟器的实验表明，该方法能有效生成多样化运动原语库，在4个机器人操作任务上优于使用推断和手工参数化的经典QD算法。

Conclusion: 本文验证了通过LLM自动设计质量和多样性指标来驱动QD算法的可行性，为机器人零样本适应部署时的约束提供了更自主的解决方案，同时保持了运动原语的多样性。

Abstract: Quality-diversity (QD) algorithms have been gaining traction in robot learning, where diverse motion primitive libraries allow robots to adapt zero-shot to constraints at deployment time. However, such methods typically require expert designers to write the success condition, fitness and diversity metrics, and this strongly limits the robot's autonomy. On the other hand, existing LLM-based reward-shaping techniques allow robots to learn autonomously but only output single high-performing solutions, limiting the robot's adaptability. In this paper, we propose an approach designed to output diverse motion primitive archives by autonomously leveraging quality-diversity algorithms, only requiring a free-form description of the task in common language. To address the difficulty of designing relevant fitness and diversity metrics, we propose an autonomous exploration mechanism able to reliably output sets of functionals covering the fitness and behavior descriptor (BD) space. First, we pose policy exploration as a functional design problem, where the functional spaces are lower-dimensional than the full BD and fitness spaces, and propose an LLM-based exploration scheme to sample from these low-dimensional spaces without any task-specific prompts, fine-tuning or expert intervention. We adapt a multi-BD variant of the MAP-Elites success (MES) algorithm, designed to leverage the heterogeneous BD samples. Finally, through experiments based on the genesis simulator, we show that our method effectively generates archives of diverse motion primitives, outperforming classical QD algorithms with inferred and hand-written parametrizations on a set of $4$ robotic manipulation tasks.

</details>


### [327] [DARP: A Calibrated Dual-Arm RGB-D-IR Dataset for Multi-View Robotic Perception](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.31002)
*Manish Kansana,Mohammed Yusuf Mujawar,Sudip Mittal,Shahram Rahimi,Noorbakhsh Amiri Golilarz*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了DARP（双臂机器人感知）数据集，一个标定过的双臂RGB-D-IR数据集，用于物体中心的机器人感知。系统使用两个独立运动的eye-in-hand机械臂分别位于共享桌面工作区两侧，每臂搭载Intel RealSense传感器连续记录RGB、深度和红外数据，同时同步记录关节状态。数据集包含10个独特桌面物体，保留了原始传感器记录、机器人状态日志、物体级元数据和标定信息。通过确定性多视角融合管线评估，224个留出测试关键帧上156万个查询点取得了2.13mm的中位点到网格距离和4.04mm的RMSE。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单视角机器人感知常受自遮挡和不完整表面可见性限制，而现有的多视角数据集在标定精度、模态丰富性以及双臂协同设置方面存在不足。需要一个支持双臂协同、多模态同步采集且具备完整标定信息的可复用数据集，以推动多视角重建、协作感知和主动感知研究。

Method: DARP采用双臂设置，两臂各携带Intel RealSense传感器，位于共享工作区两侧并独立运动。数据采集流程包括自动定位、双臂交叉确认、自适应视点生成和连续多模态记录。物体无固定位姿或标记位置。实现了一个确定性的多视角融合管线，将标定的RGB-D观测转化为互补的局部点云和实测表面网格，不使用学习或生成式补全方法。

Result: 在224个留出RGB-D关键帧（包含1,563,466个三维查询点）上评估，中位点到网格距离为2.13mm，RMSE为4.04mm，96.56%的点位于实测表面网格10mm以内，验证了数据采集的几何一致性。

Conclusion: DARP作为可复用资源，旨在支持多视角重建、协作机器人感知、多模态融合、主动感知以及基于部分物体观测的未来学习推理研究。该数据集通过严格的几何一致性评估证明了采集质量的可靠性。

Abstract: Robotic perception from a single viewpoint is often limited by self-occlusion and incomplete surface visibility. This paper presents DARP(Dual-Arm Robotic Perception) https://doi.org/10.21227/rmv3-be47, a calibrated dual-arm RGB-D-IR dataset for object-centered robotic perception using two independently moving eye-in-hand manipulators positioned on opposite sides of a shared tabletop workspace. Each arm carries an Intel RealSense sensor that continuously records RGB, depth, and stereo infrared data while synchronized robot joint states are logged for pose recovery. Objects are placed without fixed poses or marked locations, and the acquisition procedure performs automatic localization, cross-arm confirmation, adaptive viewpoint generation, and continuous multimodal recording. DARP contains ten unique tabletop objects and preserves the original sensor recordings, robot-state logs, object-level metadata, and calibration information required to reconstruct camera trajectories in a shared metric frame. To evaluate the geometric consistency of the acquisition, we implement a deterministic multi-view fusion pipeline that converts calibrated RGB-D observations into complementary partial point clouds and measured surface meshes without using learned or generative completion methods. Evaluation on 224 held-out RGB-D keyframes comprising 1,563,466 three-dimensional query points yields a median point-to-mesh distance of 2.13~mm and an RMSE of 4.04~mm, with 96.56\% of points within 10~mm of the measured-surface mesh. DARP is intended as a reusable resource for multi-view reconstruction, collaborative robotic perception, multimodal fusion, active perception, and future learning-based reasoning over partial object observations.

</details>


### [328] [SUN: Persistent Programs For Language-Grounded Control-to-Learning-to-Real Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.31167)
*Weiqi Wang,Zhi Li,Yudong Lei,David Martinez,Xiaofeng Gao,Yuxin Jiang,Chenfanfu Jiang,Yingnian Wu,Demetri Terzopoulos,Ran Gong*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了SUN Programs（语义统一程序），通过将几何和接触关系编译为MPC成本、RL奖励和策略条件等，实现模型预测控制与学习策略在长时程操作任务中的语义统一。Kuafu系统利用大视觉语言模型自动合成SUN程序，在九个任务中达到82.03%的宏成功率，数据效率提升10.57倍，并能在模拟和实体机器人上训练出高性能策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法在模型预测控制与学习策略之间存在语义割裂：控制依据明确的任务目标执行，而学习策略的奖励函数往往人工设计且丢失任务语义，导致学到的行为偏离控制验证过的行为。

Method: 提出SUN Programs（类型化可执行程序），将几何与接触关系定义一次并编译为MPC成本、满足谓词、RL奖励、转换守卫和诊断信息。Kuafu系统由大视觉语言模型驱动，从语言和场景语义中自动合成SUN程序，通过MPC进行可行性筛选，并保留语义训练阶段条件化策略。

Result: 在九个任务上达到82.03%宏成功率，优于稀疏奖励（35.67%）和阶段行为克隆（24.75%）基线；在8192路规模下，每小时人类遥操作生成成功轨迹时间提升10.57倍；500条轨迹可训练DP3策略在模拟中达46.0%成功率（对比基线22.4%），实体Franka和Kinova机器人上达34.7%。

Conclusion: 模拟验证的任务语义能够有效将控制策略摊销为鲁棒的学习策略，无需演示或手工稠密奖励，统一了符号规划与数据驱动的执行。

Abstract: Bridging model-based control and learned policies in long-horizon manipulation has harbored a silent disagreement: control executes specified objectives, learning amortizes that behavior into a reactive policy, yet existing protocols discard task semantics, leaving rewards hand-crafted and behavior drifting from what control verified.We introduce Semantically UNified (SUN) Programs, typed executables where geometric and contact relations are defined once and compiled into aligned Model Predictive Control (MPC) costs, satisfaction predicates, RL rewards, transition guards, and diagnostics. Our system, Kuafu, driven by large vision language systems, automatically synthesizes SUN Programs from language and scene semantics, screens feasibility via MPC, and retains semantics while training stage-conditioned policies. Across nine tasks, Kuafu achieves 82.03% macro-success, outperforming sparse-reward (35.67%) and Stage-BC (24.75%) baselines. At 8192-way scale, it generates 10.57x the successful trajectory time per hour of human teleoperation. With 500 trajectories per task, Kuafu data trains DP3 policies to 46.0% simulation success (vs. 22.4% for alternatives) and 34.7% on physical Franka and Kinova robots. These results establish that simulation-screened task semantics can effectively amortize control into robust policies, without demonstrations or manual dense rewards, unifying symbolic planning and data-driven execution.

</details>
